\displaystyle-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{x_{1}}B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+c(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{x_{1}}E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}J\cdot e_{3}^{\prime},

and

\partial_{r}(\alpha_{2})=\partial_{r}(ZB\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+ZE\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})-\frac{1}{c}(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}E)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}.

Since
e_{1}
commutes with
\partial_{x_{i}}
, we obtain from (
2.6a
) that

e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot(\partial_{x_{1}}B)=e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{1}\cdot\nabla)B=(e_{2}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(B\times e_{1})+(e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot e_{1})(\nabla\cdot B)
.And from (
2.6a
) , we have

(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(e_{1}\times B)=((e_{1}^{\prime}\times e_{3}^{\prime})\times\nabla)(e_{1}\times B)+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(e_{1}\times B)=(e_{2}^{\prime}\times\nabla)(B\times e_{1})+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(e_{1}\times B).

So by Lemma
2.19
, we have

\displaystyle e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot(\partial_{x_{1}}B)-(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=(e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot e_{1})(\nabla\cdot B)-e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(e_{1}\times B)=\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j,k}C_{i,j,k}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}(E_{k}),

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{1}}E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}E)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot e_{1})J_{0}+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j,k}C_{i,j,k}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}(B_{k}).

Thus we compute that

\displaystyle(\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}-\partial_{r})(\alpha_{1})=

\displaystyle(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-\partial_{r}(ZB\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+ZE\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{c}J\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{x_{1}}B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{c}(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{x_{1}}E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}(e_{1}\times\partial_{r}E)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime},

so the proof is thus finished by using (
2.54
).

3.

If
Z=\Omega_{ij}
.
We just prove for
Z=\Omega_{12}
, the cases
\Omega_{13},\Omega_{23}
are similar.

Case 1:
\xi=\rho,\sigma
.
We only proof the
\rho
term as the
\sigma
term is similar.
Remind
Z=\Omega_{12}=x_{1}\partial_{x_{2}}-x_{2}\partial_{x_{1}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc\displaystyle D^{(1)}_{Z}[E,B]=ZE+(E_{2},-E_{1},0)=ZE+E\times e_{3},\quad\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z}[E,B]=ZB+(B_{2},-B_{1},0)=ZB+B\times e_{3},

(2.58)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z=Z\partial_{t},\quad Z\partial_{x_{1}}=\partial_{x_{1}}Z-\partial_{x_{2}},\quad Z\partial_{x_{2}}=\partial_{x_{2}}Z+\partial_{x_{1}},\quad Z\partial_{x_{3}}=\partial_{x_{3}}Z,\quad Z\nabla=\nabla Z-\nabla\times e_{3}=\nabla Z+e_{3}\times\nabla.

From direct calculations, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}(ZE+E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}Z(\partial_{t}E)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}(\partial_{t}E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{c}Z(c\nabla\times B+J)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+((\nabla\times B)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+((e_{3}\times\nabla)\times B)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}J\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+((\nabla\times B)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(\nabla\times(B\times e_{3}))+\frac{1}{c}J\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+(e_{1}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(e_{3}\times B)+\frac{1}{c}J\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}.

By using (
2.53
), we compute that

\displaystyle\partial_{r}(ZE+E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle(\partial_{r}(ZE))\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}+\partial_{r}(E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot(ZE)+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j}C_{i,j}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}(ZE_{i})+\nabla\cdot(E\times e_{3})+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j,k}C_{i,j,k}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}E_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z(\nabla\cdot E)-(e_{3}\times\nabla)\cdot E+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j}C_{i,j}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}(ZE_{i})+\nabla\cdot(E\times e_{3})+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j,k}C_{i,j,k}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}E_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle ZJ_{0}+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j}C_{i,j}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}(ZE_{i})+\frac{1}{r}\sum_{i,j,k}C_{i,j,k}(\theta,\phi)\Omega_{ij}E_{k}.

So we have proved it by gathering the terms above and using (
2.50
).

Case 2:
\xi=\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}
.

Next we come to prove
\alpha_{1}
, the
\alpha_{2}
term can be proved similarly. Remind (
2.6b
).
Together with (
2.58
) and (
2.6a
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\alpha_{1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}(ZB\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}(B\times e_{3})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}(ZE\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}(E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-Z(\nabla\times E)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-((\nabla\times E)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+(Z(\nabla\times B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})+((\nabla\times B)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-((e_{3}\times\nabla)\times E)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-((\nabla\times E)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle((e_{3}\times\nabla)\times B)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+((\nabla\times B)\times e_{3})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot(\nabla\times(E\times e_{3}))+(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot(\nabla\times(B\times e_{3}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-(e_{2}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(E\times e_{3})+(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+(e_{3}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(B\times e_{3}),

and we compute

\partial_{r}\alpha_{1}=\partial_{r}(ZB)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+\partial_{r}(ZE)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+(\partial_{r}B\times e_{3})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+(\partial_{r}E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}
. Together with (
2.6a
), we have

(\partial_{r}B\times e_{3})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(B\times e_{3})=((e_{1}^{\prime}\times e_{2}^{\prime})\times\nabla)\cdot(B\times e_{3})+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(B\times e_{3})=(e_{3}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(B\times e_{3})+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(B\times e_{3}).

Similarly,
(\partial_{r}E\times e_{3})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=-(e_{2}^{\prime}\times\nabla)\cdot(E\times e_{3})+e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(E\times e_{3}).

Thus

(\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}-\partial_{r})\alpha_{1}=-(\nabla\times ZE)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+(\nabla\times ZB)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}-(\partial_{r}(ZB)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+\partial_{r}(ZE)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})-e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{2}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(B\times e_{3})-e_{1}^{\prime}\cdot(e_{3}^{\prime}\cdot\nabla)(E\times e_{3}),

so the proof is thus finished by using (
2.54
) and Lemma
2.19
.

∎

Lemma 2.23
.

For any
t\geq 0,x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},c\geq 1
satisfies that
|x|\leq 10c(1+t)
, denote
\xi=\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho,\sigma
, for
Z_{1}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}}
we have

|\xi(\mathpzc D_{Z_{1}}[E,B])|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|}\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|\xi(\mathpzc D_{Z}[E,B])|+\frac{1}{1+t+\frac{|x|}{c}}\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|.

Proof.

We first prove for
Z_{1}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
. For
\xi=\rho
, using the fact that

\partial_{t}=\frac{ct}{(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}S-\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\Omega_{i},\quad\partial_{x_{i}}=\frac{\Omega_{i}}{t}-\frac{x_{i}}{c^{2}t}\partial_{t}=\frac{\Omega_{i}}{t}-\frac{x_{i}}{ct}\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},

(2.59)

we have that

\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}E\cdot\frac{x}{r}=[\frac{t}{(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}SE+\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\Omega_{i}E]\cdot\frac{x}{r}.

Next, recall that

\rho(\mathpzc D_{\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}}[E,B])=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}E\cdot\frac{x}{r}\quad,\rho(\mathpzc D_{S}[E,B])=SE\cdot\frac{x}{r},\quad\rho(\mathpzc D_{\Omega_{1}}[E,B])=\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{1}}[E,B]\cdot\frac{x}{r}=[\Omega_{1}E+\frac{1}{c}(0,-B_{3},B_{2})]\cdot\frac{x}{r},

so we have that

\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}E\cdot\frac{x}{r}=[\frac{t}{(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\rho(\mathpzc D_{S}[E,B])-\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\rho(\mathpzc D_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B])]-[\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}(\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}E)]\cdot\frac{x}{r}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{1}}[E,B]-\Omega_{1}E=\frac{1}{c}(0,-B_{3},B_{2}),\quad\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{2}}[E,B]-\Omega_{2}E=\frac{1}{c}(B_{3},0,-B_{1}),\quad\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{3}}[E,B]-\Omega_{3}E=\frac{1}{c}(-B_{2},B_{1},0),

\displaystyle\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{\Omega_{1}}[E,B]-\Omega_{1}B=\frac{1}{c}(0,E_{3},-E_{2}),\quad\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{\Omega_{2}}[E,B]-\Omega_{2}B=\frac{1}{c}(-E_{3},0,E_{1}),\quad\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{\Omega_{3}}[E,B]-\Omega_{3}B=\frac{1}{c}(E_{2},-E_{1},0).

Thus we have

[\sum_{i}x_{i}(\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}E)]\cdot\frac{x}{r}=\frac{1}{c}(x_{2}B_{3}-x_{3}B_{2},x_{3}B_{1}-x_{1}B_{3},x_{1}B_{2}-x_{2}B_{1})\cdot\frac{x}{r}=\frac{1}{c}(x\times B)\cdot\frac{x}{r}=0,

so the proof for
\rho
is thus finished, the proof for
\sigma
can be similar.

Next we come to compute the term
\alpha_{1}
, the case of
\alpha_{2}
would be similar. Recall that
\alpha_{1}([E,B])=E\cdot e^{\prime}_{2}+B\cdot e^{\prime}_{3}
, thus still using (
2.59
), we still have that

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}}[E,B])=

\displaystyle[\frac{t}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{S}[E,B])-\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B])]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i}\frac{1}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}[x_{i}(\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}E)]\cdot e^{\prime}_{2}+[x_{i}(\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}B)]\cdot e^{\prime}_{3}.

From direct calculations, we have

\displaystyle[\sum_{i}x_{i}(\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}E)]\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{c}(x_{2}B_{3}-x_{3}B_{2},x_{3}B_{1}-x_{1}B_{3},x_{1}B_{2}-x_{2}B_{1})\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{c}r(e_{1}^{\prime}\times B)\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=-rB\cdot e_{3}^{\prime},

\displaystyle[\sum_{i}x_{i}(\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B]-\Omega_{i}B)]\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=-\frac{1}{c}(x_{2}E_{3}-x_{3}E_{2},x_{3}E_{1}-x_{1}E_{3},x_{1}E_{2}-x_{2}E_{1})\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=-\frac{1}{c}r(e_{1}^{\prime}\times E)\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=-rE\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}.

Come back to
\alpha_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}}[E,B])=

\displaystyle[\frac{t}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{S}[E,B])-\sum_{i}\frac{x_{i}}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\alpha_{1}(\mathpzc D_{\Omega_{i}}[E,B])]-\sum_{i}\frac{r}{c(t+\frac{r}{c})(t-\frac{r}{c})}\alpha_{1}([E,B]),

so the proof is thus finished for
Z=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
.

For the case
Z=\partial_{x_{i}}
, using (
2.59
) we have

\xi(\mathpzc D_{\partial_{x_{i}}}([E,B]))=\xi(\partial_{x_{i}}([E,B]))=\frac{1}{t}\xi(\Omega_{i}([E,B]))-\frac{x_{i}}{ct}\xi(\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}[E,B]))=\frac{1}{t}\xi(\Omega_{i}([E,B]))-\frac{x_{i}}{ct}(\mathpzc D_{\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}}([E,B])).

Since
r\leq 10c(1+t)
, the second term can be directly deduced from
Z=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
. For the first term, since
t\geq\frac{1}{10}\frac{|x|}{c}
, we have that

|\frac{1}{t}\xi(\Omega_{i}([E,B])|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+t+\frac{|x|}{c}}|\Omega_{i}[E,B]|\lesssim\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}\frac{1}{1+t+\frac{|x|}{c}}|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|,

and the lemma is thus proved by combining the estimates above.
∎

Lemma 2.24
.

For any
t\geq 0,x,v\in\mathbb{R}^{3},c\geq 1
and any smooth functions
E(t,x),B(t,x)
, we have that

\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|\lesssim\kappa(v)|(|B|+|E|)+\sqrt{\kappa(v)}(|\alpha([B,E])|+|\rho([B,E])|).

Proof.

Recall the definition of
e_{1}^{\prime},e_{2}^{\prime},e_{3}^{\prime}
in (
2.4
). We have
e_{1}^{\prime}\times e_{2}^{\prime}=e_{3}^{\prime}
and (
2.6d
),
thus we compute that

\displaystyle v_{0}E+v\times B=

\displaystyle v_{0}[(E\cdot e_{1}^{\prime})e_{1}^{\prime}+(E\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})e_{2}^{\prime}+(E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})e_{3}^{\prime}]+v\times[(B\cdot e_{1}^{\prime})e_{1}^{\prime}+(B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})e_{2}^{\prime}+(B\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})e_{3}^{\prime}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}[-(v\cdot e_{i+2}^{\prime})(B\cdot e_{i+1}^{\prime})+(v\cdot e_{i+1}^{\prime})(B\cdot e_{i+2}^{\prime})+v_{0}(E\cdot e_{i}^{\prime})]e_{i}^{\prime},\quad e_{i+3}^{\prime}=e_{i}^{\prime},\quad i=1,2,3.

Recall that
E_{1}\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}=\rho([E,B])
.
Thus using (
2.9
) and (
2.6c
), we have

\displaystyle|v_{0}E+v\times B|\lesssim

\displaystyle|(-(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime})(B\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})+v_{0}(E\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}))|+|(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime})(B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})+v_{0}(E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})|+v_{0}|\rho|+|v\times\frac{x}{r}|(|B|+|E|)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|(v_{0}-(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}))(B\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}+E\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})|+|(v_{0}+(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}))(B\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}-E\cdot e_{2}^{\prime})|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|(v_{0}+(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}))(B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})|+|(v_{0}-(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime}))(B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}-E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime})|+v_{0}|\rho|+|v\times\frac{x}{r}|(|B|+|E|)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle v_{0}(|\alpha([B,E])|+|\rho([B,E])|)+|v\times\frac{x}{r}|(|B|+|E|)+v_{0}\kappa(v)(|B|+|E|),

where we use the fact that
B\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}+E\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}=\alpha_{1}([E,B]),\,\,B\cdot e_{3}^{\prime}-E\cdot e_{2}^{\prime}=-\alpha_{2}([E,B]),\,\,v_{0}-(v\cdot e_{1}^{\prime})=v_{0}\kappa(v)
. Thus by (
2.9
), we have

\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|=\frac{c}{v_{0}}\frac{1}{v_{0}}|v_{0}E+v\times B||\lesssim\kappa(v)|(|B|+|E|)+\sqrt{\kappa(v)}(|\alpha([B,E])|+|\rho([B,E])|),

the proof is thus finished.
∎

2.5
Estimates of the transport operator

We recall that

T_{0}=\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x},\quad T_{F}=\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}-\left(E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B\right)\cdot\nabla_{v},\quad\hat{v}=\frac{cv}{v_{0}},

and we write
F(t,x,v,c)=E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B
. For
T_{0}
and
T_{F}
we have the following lemmas.

Lemma 2.25
.

Recall that
T(\alpha)
defined in
Definition

2.1
. Then

v_{0}T_{0}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}=\sum_{\beta<\alpha,T(\beta)=T(\alpha)}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}(v_{0}T_{0})+\hat{Z}^{\alpha}(v_{0}T_{0}).

(2.60)

Proof.

From direct calculations, we have

[v_{0}T_{0},\hat{Z}]=0,\quad\hat{Z}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}},\hat{\Omega}_{i},\hat{\Omega}_{ij},\quad[v_{0}T_{0},S]=\frac{1}{c}v_{0}T_{0},

(2.61)

and the lemma is thus proved after iterating (
2.61
).
∎

Lemma 2.26
.

Remind
\mathpzc D_{Z}
defined in Definition
2.1
. For any vector functions
F(t,x),G(t,x)
, for any
Z\in\mathbb{K}
we have

\hat{Z}((v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}f)=(v_{0}\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z}(F,G)+v\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z}(F,G))\cdot\nabla_{v}f+(v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}f.

(2.62)

Proof.

Since
\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}},S
commutes with
\partial_{v_{i}}
, the case
\hat{Z}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}},S
is direct.
For
\hat{Z}=\hat{\Omega}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{Z}[(v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}f]-(v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}f=(v_{0}ZF+v\times ZG)\cdot\nabla_{v}f+[\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{1}},v_{0}F\cdot\nabla_{v}+v\times G\cdot\nabla_{v}]f.

Moreover, recall that

(v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}=(v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}}-v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}})G_{3}+(v_{3}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{3}})G_{1}+(v_{1}\partial_{v_{3}}-v_{3}\partial_{v_{1}})G_{2},

(2.63)

we compute that

\displaystyle[\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{1}},v_{0}F\cdot\nabla_{v}+v\times G\cdot\nabla_{v}]

\displaystyle=[\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{1}},v_{0}\partial_{v_{1}}F_{1}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{2}}F_{2}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{3}}F_{3}]

\displaystyle+[\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{1}},(v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}}-v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}})G_{3}+(v_{3}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{3}})G_{1}+(v_{1}\partial_{v_{3}}-v_{3}\partial_{v_{1}})G_{2}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{c}(v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}}-v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}})F_{2}+\frac{1}{c}(v_{1}\partial_{v_{3}}-v_{3}\partial_{v_{1}})F_{3}-\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{2}}G_{3}+\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{3}}G_{2},

thus we have proved (
2.62
) for
\hat{Z}=\hat{\Omega}_{1}
. The cases
\hat{Z}=\hat{\Omega}_{2},\hat{\Omega}_{3}
are similar. Next, for
\hat{Z}=\hat{\Omega}_{12}
,
remind (
2.63
), we compute

\displaystyle[v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}},v_{0}F\cdot\nabla_{v}+v\times G\cdot\nabla_{v}]

\displaystyle=[v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}},v_{0}\partial_{v_{1}}F_{1}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{2}}F_{2}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{3}}F_{3}]

\displaystyle+[v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}},(v_{2}\partial_{v_{1}}-v_{1}\partial_{v_{2}})G_{3}+(v_{3}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{3}})G_{1}+(v_{1}\partial_{v_{3}}-v_{3}\partial_{v_{1}})G_{2}]

\displaystyle=v_{0}\partial_{v_{1}}F_{2}-v_{0}\partial_{v_{2}}F_{1}-(v_{1}\partial_{v_{3}}-v_{3}\partial_{v_{1}})G_{1}+(v_{3}\partial_{v_{2}}-v_{2}\partial_{v_{3}})G_{2},

thus we have proved (
2.62
) for
\hat{Z}=\hat{\Omega}_{12}
, and the proof is thus finished.
∎

The following lemma show that the structure of the RVMB system (
1.1
),(
1.2
) are preserved by
commutation.

Lemma 2.27
.

Suppose
(f,E,B)
solves the relativistic Vlasov-Maxwell-Boltzmann system (
1.1
),(
1.2
), then

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]

(2.64)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}(Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f,

(2.65)

and we have a more precise estimate for
T_{F}
:

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{F}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]+\sum_{|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|-1,T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)}C_{\alpha,\alpha_{2}}(v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha=\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|-1}C_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}(v_{0}\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z^{\alpha_{1}}}(E,B)+v\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z^{\alpha_{1}}}(E,B))\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha,|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|-2,\\
T(\alpha_{1})+T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)\end{subarray}}(Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f.

(2.66)

where
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3},C_{4}
are constants only depend on
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha,i,j,k
uniformly bounded from above.

Proof.

After iterating Lemma
2.26
with
F=E(t,x),G=B(t,x)
, we have that

\displaystyle\hat{Z}^{\alpha}((v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha,|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|-2,\\
T(\alpha_{1})+T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)\end{subarray}}Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|-1}C_{\alpha,\alpha_{2}}(v_{0}\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z^{\alpha_{1}}}(E,B)+v\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z^{\alpha_{1}}}(E,B))\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+(v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha,|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|-1,\\
T(\alpha_{1})+T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)\end{subarray}}Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f

\displaystyle+(v_{0}F+v\times G)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha,T(\alpha_{1})+T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)}Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f.

Using
v_{0}T_{F}(f)=v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f),v_{0}T_{0}(f)=-(v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}f+v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)

and Lemma
2.25
, we have that

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)=\sum_{\beta<\alpha,T(\beta)=T(\alpha)}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}(v_{0}T_{0}f)+\hat{Z}^{\alpha}(v_{0}T_{0}f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]+\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k}){(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f,

and we compute that

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{F}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)=

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)+v_{0}(E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|-1,T(\beta)=T(\alpha)}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}(v_{0}T_{0}f)+\hat{Z}^{\alpha}(v_{0}T_{0}f)+(v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]+\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|-1,T(\beta)=T(\alpha)}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}((v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}f)

\displaystyle+\left[(v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f-\hat{Z}^{\alpha}((v_{0}E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}f)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textnormal{r.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{Representation for T F Z alpha f},

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

We also have a lemma on the transport equation.

Lemma 2.28
.

Let
\mathfrak{g}(t,x,v),\mathfrak{h}(t,x,v)
be two smooth non-negative functions, and there exists a uniform constant
C>0
, such that

T_{F}(\mathfrak{g})\leq C\left(\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(1+t)}\mathfrak{g}+\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}\mathfrak{g}\right)+\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(1+t)}\mathfrak{h},\quad\kappa(x,v):=\kappa(v)=1-\frac{\hat{v}}{c}\cdot\frac{x}{r},\quad\forall t,x,v,c

then for any
t\geq 0
, we have

\|\mathfrak{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\leq e^{6C}[\|\mathfrak{g}(0,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+3\|\mathfrak{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}].

(2.67)

Proof.

The case
c=1
is proved in
[
5
]
. The proof is similar, we write here for completeness. Remind that
F=E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B
. Denote
(X(t),V(t))
the characteristic of
T_{F}
satisfying

\frac{\textnormal{d}}{\textnormal{d}t}X(t)=-\hat{V}(t),\quad\frac{\textnormal{d}}{\textnormal{d}t}V(t)=-F(t),\quad X(0)=x,\quad V(0)=v.

Then using characteristic method, we can easily have that

\mathfrak{g}(t-t^{\prime},X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime})))=\mathfrak{g}(t,x,v)-\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}T_{F}(\mathfrak{g})(t-z,X(z),V(z))\textnormal{d}z,\quad\forall t^{\prime}\leq t.

cChoose
t^{\prime}=t
, we have

\mathfrak{g}(0,X(t),V(t)))=\mathfrak{g}(t,x,v)-\int_{0}^{t}T_{F}(\mathfrak{g})(t-z,X(z),V(z))\textnormal{d}z.

Denote that

\phi_{1}(t^{\prime})=(1+t^{\prime})^{-1}\log^{-2}(3+t^{\prime}),\quad\phi_{2}(t^{\prime})=\kappa(X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime}))\left(1+\left|t-t^{\prime}-\frac{X(t^{\prime})}{c}\right|\right)^{-1}\log^{-2}\left(3+\left|t-t^{\prime}-\frac{X(t^{\prime})}{c}\right|\right),

then
\int_{0}^{\infty}\phi_{1}(t^{\prime})\textnormal{d}t^{\prime}\leq 3
, and for any
x,v
, after changing of variable
\mathpzc s=t-t^{\prime}-\frac{|X(t^{\prime})|}{c}
, we have

\frac{\textnormal{d}\mathpzc s}{\textnormal{d}t^{\prime}}=-1+\frac{\hat{V}(t^{\prime})\cdot X(t^{\prime})}{c|X(t^{\prime})|}=-\kappa(X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime})).

Thus we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{2}(t^{\prime})\textnormal{d}t^{\prime}=\int_{t-\frac{|x|}{c}}^{\mathpzc s(t)}\frac{1}{(1+|\mathpzc s|)\log^{2}(3+|\mathpzc s|)}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc s\leq\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(1+|\mathpzc s|)\log^{2}(3+|\mathpzc s|)}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc s\leq 6,

which implies that

\mathfrak{g}(t,x,v)\leq\|\mathfrak{g}(0,X(t),V(t))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathfrak{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\phi_{1}(t-t^{\prime})\textnormal{d}t^{\prime}+C\int_{0}^{t}[\phi_{1}(t-t^{\prime})+\phi_{2}(t^{\prime},X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime}))]\mathfrak{g}(t-t^{\prime},X(t^{\prime}),V(t^{\prime}))\textnormal{d}t^{\prime},

and (
2.67
) is proved by Grönwall’s lemma.
∎

3
Bootstrap assumptions and basic convergence rate of distribution function

We initiate the bootstrap argument by establishing our primary assumptions. In this section, by leveraging the technical lemmas derived in the preceding sections, we obtain improved decay and regularity estimates for the distribution function
f
.

3.1
Bootstrap assumptions

Based on a standard local well-posedness argument, there exists a unique maximal solution
(f,E,B)
to the RVMB system (
1.1
)–(
1.2
) emanating from the given initial data.
1
1

1

If the initial data is of size
\epsilon_{0}
, then the lifespan of the solution is at least
\epsilon_{0}^{-1/2}
. For a detailed derivation, we refer the reader to Section 4.5 of
[
54
]
.
Let
T_{\text{max}}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\cup\{+\infty\}
denote the maximal time such that the solution is defined on the interval
[0,T_{\text{max}})
. By continuity, there exists a largest time
T\in[0,T_{\text{max}})
and a uniform constant
M_{1}
such that the following bootstrap assumptions hold for all
(t,x)\in[0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
:

|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(t,x)\leq\frac{M_{1}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)},\quad\forall|\gamma|\leq N-1,

(BA1)

and

|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(t,x)+|\nabla_{x}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(t,x)\leq\frac{M_{1}\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}},\quad\forall|\gamma|\leq N-1,

(BA2)

as well as

\sum_{i=0}^{3}|J_{i}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)|=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|+\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{v}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\leq\frac{M_{1}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}},\quad\forall|\beta|\leq N-2,

(BA3)

where we recall that
J_{i}
is defined in (
1.3
) and we assume
M_{1}\vcentcolon=C_{\textnormal{bo}}M
, where
C_{\textnormal{bo}}
is a universal constant to be determined later.
Under these assumptions we first have that

Corollary 3.1
.

Under the assumptions (
BA1
)-(
BA3
), for any
|\gamma|\leq N
, we have

|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(t,x)\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}\langle v\rangle^{8}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4},

(3.1)

and for any
|\gamma|\leq N-1
,

|\partial_{x_{i}}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{8}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4},

(3.2)

Proof.

This is the direct result of
|Z|\lesssim(1+t+|x|)(|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}|+|\nabla_{x}|)
for all
Z
and (
2.10
).
∎

Lemma 3.2
.

Denote that
F=[E,B]
or
F=\mathpzc D_{Z}(E,B)
with
Z=S,\Omega_{i},\Omega_{ij}
. Under the assumption (
BA1
)-(
BA3
) , denote also
\xi=\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho,\sigma
, we have a better estimate for
\xi(F)
:

|\xi(F)|\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}.

Proof.

First, under the assumption (
BA1
), if
\frac{|x|}{c}\geq 2(1+t)
or
\frac{|x|}{c}\leq\frac{1}{2}(1+t)
, we have

\sum_{|z|\leq 1}|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|\lesssim M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})},

so we consider the case
\frac{1}{2}(1+t)\leq\frac{|x|}{c}\leq 2(1+t)
. Denote that
u=t-\frac{r}{c},u^{\prime}=t+\frac{r}{c}
, we have that
t=\frac{u+u^{\prime}}{2},r=\frac{c(u^{\prime}-u)}{2}
, then we define that

\phi(u,u^{\prime},\omega):=\xi(F)(\frac{u+u^{\prime}}{2},\frac{c(u^{\prime}-u)}{2}\omega),\quad\xi=\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\sigma,\rho,

where
\phi
is defined on the region
u\leq u^{\prime},u^{\prime}\geq 0
. Then we have that

\xi(F)(t,r\omega)=\phi(t-\frac{r}{c},t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)=\phi(-t-\frac{r}{c},t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)+\int_{-t-\frac{r}{c}}^{t-\frac{r}{c}}\partial_{u}\phi(z,t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)\textnormal{d}z.

For the first term, using (
1.6
) we easily have that

\phi(-t-\frac{r}{c},t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)=\xi(F)(0,(ct+r)\omega)\lesssim\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(0,(ct+r)\omega)\lesssim M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})},

for all
z\in[-t-\frac{r}{c},t-\frac{r}{c}]
, we have

\phi(z,t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)=\xi(F)(\frac{t+\frac{r}{c}+z}{2},\frac{c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)}{2}\omega)
.
Since
\frac{r}{c}\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+t)
, for all
z\in[-t-\frac{r}{c},t-\frac{r}{c}]
,

c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)\geq 2r\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+t+r),\quad c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)\geq 2r\geq c,\quad(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)\geq\frac{2r}{c}\geq\frac{1}{4}(1+t+\frac{r}{c}),

thus using Lemma
2.21
and Lemma
2.22
we have that

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}-c\partial_{r}]\xi(F)=\frac{c}{r}\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 2}\sum_{k=1}^{3}[C_{\gamma,k,1}Z^{\gamma}E_{k}+C_{\gamma,k,2}Z^{\gamma}B_{k}]+c\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}C_{j,\gamma}J_{j}(\hat{Z}^{\gamma}f)

since
\partial_{u}\phi=(\partial_{t}-c\partial_{r})\xi
, by (
BA1
) and (
BA3
) we compute that

\displaystyle|\partial_{u}\phi(z,t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)|=\left|(\partial_{t}-c\partial_{r})\xi(F)(\frac{t+\frac{r}{c}+z}{2},\frac{c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)}{2}\omega)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\frac{c}{c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)}\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}[E,B]|(\frac{t+\frac{r}{c}+z}{2},\frac{c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)}{2}\omega)+c\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|J(\hat{Z}^{\gamma}f)|(\frac{t+\frac{r}{c}+z}{2},\frac{c(t+\frac{r}{c}-z)}{2}\omega))

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{r}{c})(1+t+r)}\frac{1}{1+|z|}+M_{1}\frac{c}{(c(1+t+\frac{r}{c}))^{3}}\lesssim M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{r}{c})(1+t+r)}\frac{1}{1+|z|}+M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{2}(1+t+\frac{r}{c})},

and since
\frac{r}{c}\leq 2(1+t)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-t-\frac{r}{c}}^{t-\frac{r}{c}}\partial_{u}\phi(z,t+\frac{r}{c},\omega)\textnormal{d}z\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{r}{c})(1+t+r)}\int_{-t-\frac{r}{c}}^{t-\frac{r}{c}}\frac{1}{1+|z|}\textnormal{d}|z|+M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t+\frac{r}{c})}{(1+t+\frac{r}{c})(1+t+r)}\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+\frac{r}{c})(1+t+r)},

the lemma is thus proved.
∎

From
Lemma

2.23
and
Lemma

2.24
we have the following two colloraries.

Corollary 3.3
.

Under the assumption (
BA1
)-(
BA3
), for any
t\geq 0,x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},c\geq 1
, denote
\xi=\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\rho,\sigma
, for
Z_{1}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}}
we have

|\xi(\mathpzc D_{Z_{1}}[E,B])|\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}.

Proof.

The case
|x|\geq 10c(1+t)
, is direct from Assumption (
BA1
) and (
BA2
). For
|x|\leq 10c(1+t)
, by Lemma
2.23
and Lemma
3.2
we have that

|\xi(\mathpzc D_{Z_{1}}[E,B])|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|}\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|\xi(\mathpzc D_{Z}[E,B])|+\frac{1}{1+t+\frac{|x|}{c}}\sum_{|\gamma|\leq 1}|Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|\lesssim M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)},

this completes the proof of corollary.
∎

Corollary 3.4
.

Under the assumption (
BA1
)-(
BA3
), for
Z=\Omega_{i},\Omega_{i,j},S
, we have that

\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|+\frac{c}{v_{0}}|\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z}(E,B)+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z}(E,B)|\lesssim M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})},

(3.3)

and for
Z=\partial_{x_{i}},\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
we have that

\frac{c}{v_{0}}|\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z}(E,B)+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z}(E,B)|\lesssim M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{r}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}+M_{1}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

(3.4)

Proof.

Under the assumption (
BA1
)-(
BA3
), by Lemma
3.2
, Lemma
2.24
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+M_{1}\sqrt{\kappa(v)}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})},

and the
\mathpzc D_{Z}([E,B])
term can be proved similarly.
For the
\partial_{x_{i}}
term, by Corollary
3.3
, Lemma
2.24
and Assumption (
BA2
) we have that

\displaystyle\frac{c}{v_{0}^{2}}|v_{0}\partial_{x_{i}}E+v\times\partial_{x_{i}}B|\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}+M_{1}\sqrt{\kappa(v)}\frac{\log(3+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}+M_{1}\frac{\log^{2}(3+t+\frac{|x|}{c})}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{2}},

and
\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
can be proved similarly. The proof is thus finished since
\mathpzc D_{Z}([E,B])=Z[E,B]
for
Z=\partial_{x_{i}},\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
.
∎

3.2
Improvement of the bootstrap assumptions on the distribution function

We are now ready to improve the bootstrap assumption on the distribution function
f
. We start by proving the following lemma about
\hat{Z}^{\beta}f
in phase space.

Lemma 3.5
.

Recall weight function
\mathpzc W
defined in (
2.2
). For any
M_{1}>0
fixed under Assumptions (
BA1
) to (
BA3
) , for any
t\geq 0,x,v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
|\beta|\leq N
, for any
k\leq 300
, there exist a constant
\epsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
such that for any
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,\epsilon_{0})
, if the initial data satisfies (
1.6
), we have that

\mathpzc W^{100}_{k}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\leq C_{1}\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\log^{3H(\beta)+3k}(3+t).

where
C_{1},D
are two universal constant that does not depend on
M_{1},\epsilon_{1},M,\epsilon_{0}
and
\epsilon_{0}
satisfies
\epsilon_{0}e^{DM_{1}}\leq 1
we also recall that
H(\beta)(T(\beta))
as the numbers of
S,\hat{\Omega}_{i},\hat{\Omega}_{ij}
(respectively
\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}}
) composing
\hat{Z}^{\beta}
.

Proof.

Denote
k_{0}=10^{5}
. We define that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(f)(t,x,v)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq N}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{4(k_{0}-20|\beta|)+50}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3H(\beta)}(3+t)}+\sum_{|\beta|\leq N}\frac{\mathpzc W^{4(k_{0}-20|\beta|)+50}_{k_{0}-20|\beta|}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3H(\beta)+3(k_{0}-20|\beta|)}(3+t)}+\sum_{|\beta|\leq N}\frac{\mathpzc W^{2(k_{0}-20|\beta|)+20}_{k_{0}-20|\beta|+10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3H(\beta)+3(k_{0}-20|\beta|+10)}(3+t)},

We also need to make the priori assumption that

|\mathcal{E}(f)|(t,x,v)\leq\sqrt{\epsilon_{1}}(1+t)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{12}},

(3.5)

we will then prove that

T_{F}(\mathcal{E}(f))\lesssim\frac{M_{1}}{(1+t)\log^{2}(1+t)}\mathcal{E}(f)+M_{1}\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}\mathcal{E}(f)+\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(1+t)}\epsilon_{1},

then by Lemma
2.28
the theorem is thus proved.
Since
T_{F}(\log^{-1}(3+t))\leq 0
, we compute that for any
p,q\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
,

\displaystyle T_{F}\left(\frac{\mathpzc W^{p}_{q}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3q+3H(\beta)}(3+t)}\right)\leq

\displaystyle pT_{F}(\langle v\rangle)\frac{\mathpzc W^{p-1}_{q}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3q+3H(\beta)}(3+t)}+qT_{F}(\langle z\rangle)\frac{\mathpzc W^{p}_{q-1}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3q+3H(\beta)}(3+t)}+T_{F}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)\frac{\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}{|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|}\frac{\mathpzc W^{p}_{q}}{\log^{3q+3H(\beta)}(3+t)}.

For the first term, since
\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle)\lesssim 1
, using (
3.3
) we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}T_{F}(\langle v\rangle)=\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}|(E+c^{-1}\hat{v}\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle))|\lesssim\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|\lesssim M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+M_{1}\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{2}},

so the first term is proved. For the second term, recall that
T_{0}(x-t\hat{v})=0
and
|\nabla_{v}(\hat{v})|\lesssim\frac{c}{v_{0}}
, using (
3.3
), we have

\displaystyle|T_{F}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle)|=

\displaystyle|\frac{(x-t\hat{v})\cdot T_{F}(x-t\hat{v})}{\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle}|\lesssim|T_{F}(x-t\hat{v})|\lesssim|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B||\nabla_{v}(x-t\hat{v})|\lesssim t|\nabla_{v}(\hat{v})||E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{tc}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|\lesssim M_{1}\kappa(v)\frac{t}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+M_{1}\frac{t\log(3+t)}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{2}}.

For any
a>0
we use the Young inequality

\displaystyle\frac{\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{a-1}}{\log^{3a}(3+t)}\lesssim\frac{\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{a}}{\log^{3}(3+t)\log^{3a}(3+t)}+\frac{1}{\log^{3}(3+t)}\lesssim\frac{1}{\log^{3}(3+t)}\left(1+\frac{\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{a}}{\log^{3a}(3+t)}\right),

so the second term is thus proved. For the third term, the lower order case, remind

\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{i}}=\hat{\Omega}_{i}-t\partial_{x_{i}}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}x_{i}\partial_{t}
.
Using (
2.27
), the
T_{F}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)
term is bounded by
T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}
, where

\displaystyle T_{1}=\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}|\frac{1}{v_{0}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]|,

(3.6)

\displaystyle T_{2}=\sum_{|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|-1,T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)}\frac{c}{v_{0}}|E+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times B|\left[|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right],

\displaystyle T_{3}=\sum_{\alpha=\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|-1}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\left|\mathpzc D^{(1)}_{Z^{\alpha}_{1}}(E,B)+\frac{v}{v_{0}}\times\mathpzc D^{(2)}_{Z^{\alpha}_{1}}(E,B)\right|\left[|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right],

\displaystyle T_{4}=\frac{c}{v_{0}}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha,|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|-2,T(\alpha_{1})+T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)}|Z^{\alpha_{1}}[E,B]|\left[|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right].

We first estimate the
T_{4}
term. Since
|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|-2
, we have that
H(\alpha_{2})\leq H(\alpha)-2
, then using (
3.1
) and
\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{1+t+|x|}\lesssim\frac{\log(3+t)}{1+t}
and using that
H(\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\alpha_{2})+1\leq H(\alpha)-1,H(\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\alpha_{2})\leq H(\alpha)-2
we have that for any
a,b\geq 0
,

\displaystyle T_{4}\frac{\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f}{|\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f|}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}\mathpzc W^{8}_{4}\left(|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right)\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}\frac{1}{\log^{3}(3+t)}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}_{b-20(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)}(3+t)}\left(\frac{|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|}{\log^{3(H(\alpha_{2})+1)}(3+t)}+\frac{(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)}{\log^{3(H(\alpha_{2})+1)}(3+t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{E}(f)\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}.

And the same thing holds if we replace
\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}
by
\mathpzc W^{a-40|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}
. For the term
T_{1}
, using (
5.52
) and by
Theorem

5.12
we have that for any
\lambda<\frac{5+\gamma}{3}
we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathpzc W^{4(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+50}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|}+\mathpzc W^{2(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+20}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|+10}\right|T_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|}\left|\mathpzc W^{4(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+50}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|}+\mathpzc W^{2(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+20}_{k_{0}+10-20|\alpha|}\right||Q_{c}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f,\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha||}(1+t)^{-\lambda}[\mathpzc W^{4(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+50}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathpzc W^{2(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+20}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|+10}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]

\displaystyle\times[\|\mathpzc W^{4(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+50}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathpzc W^{2(k_{0}-20|\alpha|)+20}_{k_{0}-20|\alpha|+10}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\lambda}\|\mathcal{E}^{2}(f)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-\frac{8+\gamma}{6}}.

For the
T_{2}
term, first since either
|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|\leq t
or
|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|\geq t
, we have that

\frac{1}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+t)}\lesssim\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{1}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)},

and since
|\alpha_{2}|<|\alpha|
and
T(\alpha_{2})=T(\alpha)
, we have
H(\alpha_{2})<H(\alpha)
, recall that
\kappa(v)\lesssim 1
, using that
H(\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\alpha_{2})+1\leq H(\alpha),H(\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}})=H(\alpha_{2})\leq H(\alpha)-1
and (
3.3
) we have for any
a,b\geq 0
,

\displaystyle T_{2}\frac{\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f}{|\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f|}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\left(\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{2}}\right)\left(|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right)\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\left(\kappa(v)\frac{1}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{2}}\right)\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}_{b-20(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|}{\log^{3(H(\alpha_{2})+1)}(3+t)}+\frac{1}{\log^{3}(3+t)}\frac{(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)}{\log^{3\left(H(\alpha_{2})\right)}(3+t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{E}(f)\left(\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}+\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{E}(f)\left(\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}\right).

For the term
T_{3}
with
\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}=S,\hat{\Omega}_{i},\hat{\Omega}_{ij}
, we still have
H(\alpha_{2})<H(\alpha)
, so the proof is the same as
T_{2}
case. For
\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}}
, this time
H(\alpha_{2})=H(\alpha)
, using (
3.4
) we have that for any
a,b\geq 0
,

\displaystyle T_{3}\frac{\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f}{|\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f|}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80|\alpha|}_{b-20|\alpha|}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)+3H(\alpha)}(3+t)}\left(\frac{\kappa(v)\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\frac{\mathpzc W^{a-80(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}_{b-20(|\alpha_{2}|+1)}}{\log^{3\left(b-20|\alpha|\right)}(3+t)}\left(\frac{\kappa(v)\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+t+\frac{|x|}{c})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{\log^{3}(3+t)|\hat{\Omega}_{i}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|}{\log^{3(H(\alpha_{2})+1)}(3+t)}+\frac{(t+\frac{|x|}{c})(|\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|+|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|)}{\log^{3H(\alpha_{2})}(3+t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{1}\mathcal{E}(f)\left(\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\kappa(v)}{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)\log^{2}(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}\right),

by using Lemma
2.28
we get our result, which also close the priori assumption (
3.5
) by assuming

C_{1}\sqrt{\epsilon_{0}}e^{DM_{1}}\leq\frac{1}{2},

(3.7)

here
C
is a universal constant, and the proof is thus finished.
∎

We note that in the following two sections we also need to assume (
3.7
) holds to use Theorem
3.5
. Next we come to prove that

Lemma 3.6
.

For any
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, for any
t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\langle v\rangle^{30}|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}(1+t)^{-\frac{8+\gamma}{6}}.

(3.8)

As a consequence,

\langle v\rangle^{30}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}.

(3.9)

Proof.

First, recall that
T_{0}=\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}
, from integration by parts, we have

\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{0}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x,

and using (
2.64
) and (
5.52
) we have that

\displaystyle v_{0}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|}C_{\alpha,\beta}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]+\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}(Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{1}E_{k}+C_{2}B_{k})v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f+Z^{\alpha_{1}}(C_{3}E_{k}+C_{4}B_{k})(v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}-v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}})\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f.

Using that

\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{i}}=\hat{\Omega}_{i}-t\partial_{x_{i}}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}x_{i}\partial_{t}=\hat{\Omega}_{i}-t\partial_{x_{i}}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}(x_{i}-\hat{v}_{i}t)\partial_{t}-\frac{1}{c}\hat{v}_{i}S+\frac{1}{c^{2}}\hat{v}_{i}\sum_{j}x_{j}\partial_{x_{j}},

then using integration by parts in
x
for
\partial_{x_{i}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|+1,|\alpha_{1}|\leq|\alpha|}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{2}|Z^{\alpha_{1}}[E,B](t,x)||\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x\right|

(3.10)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(t+\frac{|x|}{c})|\nabla_{x}Z^{\alpha_{1}}[E,B](t,x)||\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|\leq|\alpha|}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{c}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f,\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f)|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x\right|\vcentcolon=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}.

Using Lemma
5.12
and Lemma
3.5
we have that

\mathpzc W^{30}_{10}|Q_{c}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f,\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f)|(t,x,v)\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{5+\gamma}{3}}\|\mathpzc W^{50}_{50}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\|\mathpzc W^{50}_{50}\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{8+\gamma}{6}}\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}},

(3.11)

together with that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{-5}dx\lesssim 1

we have

T_{3}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{8+\gamma}{6}}\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}.

(3.12)

Recall that
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, for
T_{1}
we use (
3.1
), for
T_{2}
we use (
3.2
), and by Lemma
3.5
we compute that

\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\log\left(3+\left|t-\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}\right|\right)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}\frac{\log^{1000}(3+t)}{\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}}\textnormal{d}x\right|

(3.13)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{5+\gamma}{3}}\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{-10}\textnormal{d}x\right|\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{5+\gamma}{3}}\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}.

We plug (
3.12
) and (
3.13
) into (
3.10
) to obtain (
3.8
). To show (
3.9
),
we integrate from
0
to
t
and obtain that

\langle v\rangle^{30}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}x|\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{30}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(0,x,v)\textnormal{d}x|+\int_{0}^{t}\langle v\rangle^{30}|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t^{\prime},x,v)\textnormal{d}x|\textnormal{d}t^{\prime}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}+\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}},

and the lemma is thus proved.
∎

Lemma 3.7
.

Let
N_{x}>7
. For any
|\beta|\leq N
, for any
t\in[0,T],x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have that

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log^{100000}(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}.

(3.14)

Moreover we have a better decay depending on different cases

\forall|x|\leq ct,\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(1+t)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}{(1+t+|x|)^{5}},

(3.15)

and

\forall|x|\geq ct,\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{8}}.

(3.16)

Proof.

First, to prove (
3.14
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\overset{\eqref{main inequality from 1 t - r c to 1 + t + r-2}}{\lesssim}

\displaystyle\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{40}_{20}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|

(3.17)

\displaystyle\overset{\textnormal{Lemma}\ref{L 1 L infty estimate on x t v}}{\lesssim}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\|\mathpzc W^{50}_{30}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\overset{\textnormal{Lemma}\ref{main estimate for f}}{\lesssim}\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log^{100000}(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}.

If
|x|\leq ct
, using (
2.10
) and similarly as (
3.17
),
we have that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{5}}{(1+t+|x|)^{5}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{40}_{20}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}{(1+t+|x|)^{5}}\|\mathpzc W^{50}_{30}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}{\lesssim}\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log^{10000}(1+t)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}{(1+t+|x|)^{5}}.

If
|x|\geq ct
, using
Lemma

2.9
we have that

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{20}_{10}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}}{(1+t+|x|)^{8}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc W^{40}_{20}|\hat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{8}},

the proof is thus finished.
∎

Corollary 3.8
.

For any
|\beta|\leq N
, for any
t\in[0,T],x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have that

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{40}}{v_{0}}\left|\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]\right|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{4+\frac{2+\gamma}{12}}}.

(3.18)

Proof.

Using
5.10
and (
5.52
) we have for any
p>\frac{3}{3+\gamma}
,

\textnormal{l.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{eq:basic estimate for Q f f dv-1}\lesssim(1+t)^{-3-\frac{3}{p}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\|\mathpzc W^{k+55}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{1}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\mathpzc W^{k+50}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

If
|x|\leq ct
, from
Equation

2.10
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-3-\frac{3}{p}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\|\mathpzc W^{k+55}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{1}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\mathpzc W^{k+50}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left(1+t-\frac{|x|}{c}\right)^{3+\frac{3}{p}}}{(1+t)^{3+\frac{3}{p}}(1+t+|x|)^{3+\frac{3}{p}}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\|\mathpzc W^{k+71}_{12}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{1}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\mathpzc W^{k+66}_{12}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}}{(1+t+|x|)^{4+\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}}\cdot(1+t)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{3}}\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}}{(1+t+|x|)^{4+\frac{2+\gamma}{12}}}.

If
|x|\geq ct
, from
Lemma

2.9
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\|\mathpzc W^{k+55}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{1}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\mathpzc W^{k+50}_{4}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t+|x|)^{-5}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\|\mathpzc W^{k+65}_{9}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{1}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\mathpzc W^{k+60}_{9}|\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f|(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t+|x|)^{-5}\cdot\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}(1+t)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{4+\frac{2+\gamma}{12}}}.

So the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 3.9
.

For any smooth function
h
, for any
t\geq 0,x,v\in\mathbb{R}^{3},c\geq 1
satisfy that
|x|\leq ct
, we have that

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}t^{3}h(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\textnormal{d}v-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}h(t,y,\frac{\check{x}}{t})\textnormal{d}y\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|h|\right)(t,y,v)+\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|\right)\nabla_{v}h|(t,y,v).

(3.19)

Proof.

Recall (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{l.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{eq:smoothg1}=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{|y-x|<ct}\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}h(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x-y}{t}})\textnormal{d}y-\int_{|x-y|\leq ct}\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}h(t,y,\frac{\check{x}}{t})\textnormal{d}y\right)-\int_{|x-y|\geq ct}\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}h(t,y,\frac{\check{x}}{t})\textnormal{d}y:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

For the term
I_{1}
, we have

|\partial_{y_{j}}\check{y}_{i}|=\left|\frac{\delta_{ij}}{\sqrt{1-\frac{|y|^{2}}{c^{2}}}}+\frac{y_{i}y_{j}}{c^{2}(1-\frac{|y|^{2}}{c^{2}})^{3/2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\frac{|y|^{2}}{c^{2}})^{3/2}}=\left(\frac{c^{2}+\check{y}^{2}}{c^{2}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}=\left(\frac{v_{0}}{c}\right)^{3}(\hat{y}).

From mean value theorem, we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq ct}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\textnormal{d}y\cdot\frac{1}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|h|\right)(t,y,v)+\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|\nabla_{v}h|\right)(t,y,v)

(3.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|h|\right)(t,y,v)+\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|\nabla_{v}h|\right)(t,y,v).

For
I_{2}
, denote
v=\frac{\check{x}}{t}
. From
|x|\leq ct
, we have

c^{2}=v_{0}^{2}(1-\frac{|x|^{2}}{c^{2}t^{2}})=v_{0}^{2}(\frac{c^{2}t^{2}-|x|^{2}}{c^{2}t^{2}})\leq v_{0}^{2}(\frac{|y|(ct+|x|)}{c^{2}t^{2}})\leq\frac{2v_{0}^{2}|y|}{ct},

which implies that

I_{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{ct}\sup_{y,v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left({\left|\frac{v_{0}}{c}\right|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}\left|h\right|}\right)(t,y,v)\int_{|x-y|\geq ct}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\frac{1}{ct}\sup_{y,v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left({\left|\frac{v_{0}}{c}\right|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}\left|h\right|}\right)(t,y,v),

(3.21)

and the lemma is thus proved after gathering (
3.20
) and (
3.21
).
∎

Lemma 3.10
.

Let
h(t,x,v)=f(t,x+t\hat{v},v),z_{i}(t,x,v)=x_{i}-t\hat{v}_{i}
, then

|\langle v\rangle^{b}\langle x\rangle^{a}h(t,x,v)|\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{b}(|\langle z\rangle^{a}f|)(t,x+t\hat{v},v),\quad\frac{v_{0}}{c}|\nabla_{v}h(t,x,v)|\lesssim\sum_{|\gamma|=1}(|\langle z\rangle\hat{Z}^{\gamma}f|)(t,x+t\hat{v},v).

Proof.

The first inequality just follows by
z_{i}(t,x+t\hat{v},v)=x_{i}+t\hat{v}_{i}-t\hat{v}_{i}=x_{i}
. For the second, we compute that

\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}g=\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}f+t\partial_{x_{j}}f-t\sum_{i}\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{v_{0}^{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}f,

then using that
\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}=\hat{\Omega}_{j}-t\partial_{x_{j}}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}x_{j}\partial_{t}
and
\hat{v}=\frac{cv}{v_{0}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}+t\partial_{x_{j}}-\sum_{i}\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{v_{0}^{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}=\hat{\Omega}_{j}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}x_{j}\partial_{t}-t\sum_{i}\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{v_{0}^{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}=\hat{\Omega}_{j}-\frac{1}{c}(x_{j}-t\hat{v}_{j})\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}-\frac{1}{c^{2}}\hat{v}_{j}t\partial_{t}-\frac{t}{c^{2}}\sum_{i}\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}\partial_{x_{i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\Omega}_{j}-\frac{1}{c}(x_{j}-t\hat{v}_{j})\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}-\frac{1}{c}\hat{v}_{j}S+\sum_{i}\frac{1}{c^{2}}\hat{v}_{j}x_{i}\partial_{x_{i}}-\frac{t}{c^{2}}\sum_{i}\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}_{j}\partial_{x_{i}}=\hat{\Omega}_{j}-\frac{1}{c}(x_{j}-t\hat{v}_{j})\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}-\frac{1}{c}\hat{v}_{j}S+\frac{\hat{v}_{j}}{c^{2}}\sum_{i}(x_{i}-t\hat{v}_{i})\partial_{x_{i}},

so the proof follows.
∎

Lemma 3.11
.

For any
|\alpha|\leq N-1
,
and
\Psi(\omega,v),\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
is sufficient smooth and satisfies that

\|\Psi(\omega,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}+\|\nabla_{v}\Psi(\omega,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{10},

(3.22)

then for any
t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v|\leq C_{4}\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+|t|+|x|)^{3}}.

(3.23)

where
C_{4}
is a universal constant.

Proof.

First we have if
t\leq\max\left(1,\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}\right)
, then the result follows by (
3.14
) and (
3.16
). For the case
|x|\leq ct
, if
\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(1+t)(1+|t-\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}|)^{2}\leq(1+t+|x|)^{2}
, then result follows by (
3.15
).
we now come to prove the case

t\geq\max\left(1,\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}\right),\quad\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(1+t)(1+|t-\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}|)^{2}\geq(1+t+|x|)^{2}.

Then we have

1\lesssim\frac{\log^{\frac{9}{2}\left(N_{x}+N\right)}(1+t)(1+|t-\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}|)^{3}}{(1+t+\left|x\right|)^{3}}\lesssim\frac{\log^{\frac{9}{2}\left(N_{x}+N\right)}(1+t)(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+\left|x\right|)^{3}}.

(3.24)

Denote that
g(t,x,v)=\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x+t\hat{v},v)
. Since
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, first by Lemma
3.10
and Lemma
3.5
we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|g|\right)(t,y,v)+\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|\nabla_{v}g|\right)(t,y,v)

(3.25)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\Psi(\omega,v)|+|\nabla_{v}\Psi(\omega,v)|\right)\langle v\rangle^{20}\langle z\rangle^{15}\sum_{\gamma\leq 1}|Z^{\gamma}Z^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}(1+t)^{\frac{2+\gamma}{12}}.

Then from (
3.19
), (
3.24
) and (
3.25
), we have

\displaystyle\left|t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|=\left|t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g(t,x-t\hat{v},v)\textnormal{d}v|\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}g)(t,y,\frac{\check{x}}{t})\textnormal{d}y\right|+\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\frac{\log^{\frac{9}{2}\left(N_{x}+N\right)}(1+t)}{t}\left[\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|g|\right)(t,y,v)+\left(|\frac{v_{0}}{c}|^{10}\langle y\rangle^{10}|\nabla_{v}g|\right)(t,y,v)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\frac{v_{0}^{5}}{c^{5}}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)(t,y+x,\frac{\check{x}}{t})\textnormal{d}y\right|+\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\langle\frac{\check{x}}{t}\rangle^{-10}+\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\langle\frac{|x|}{t}\rangle^{-10}+\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{(1+t)^{3}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}},

so the lemma is thus proved.
∎

Next we come to give a better estimate for
\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x}
.

Lemma 3.12
.

For any
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, and
\Psi(\omega,v),\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
is sufficient regular and satisfies (
3.22
).
Then for any
t\in R^{+},v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|+\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+|t|+|x|)^{4_{-}}}.

Proof.

This is obvious by (
3.15
) and (
3.16
) if
\left|x\right|\geq ct
or
|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|\leq 1
, so we only need to prove the case
t-\frac{\left|x\right|}{c}\geq 1
. Using (
2.59
) we have that

(t-\frac{|x|}{c})\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|+|\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\right]\lesssim\sum_{|\gamma|=1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)Z^{\gamma}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|,

(3.26)

and we need to recall that
Z=\hat{Z}
if
Z=\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}},S
and
Z=\hat{\Omega}_{i}-\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}
, if
Z=\Omega_{i}
and
Z=\hat{\Omega}_{ij}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}+v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}
if
Z=\Omega_{ij}
, so we can just finish the proof by integration by parts in
v
and (
3.15
).
∎

Corollary 3.13
.

For any
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, and
\Phi(\omega,v)
is sufficient regular that satisfies (
3.22
), we have

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[Q_{c}(f,f)](t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|+\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[Q_{c}(f,f)](t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{5+}}.

(3.27)

Proof.

Recall (
5.52
). If
|x|\geq ct
, then we obtain (
3.27
) from
Lemma

5.11
and (
3.16
), If
\left|t-\frac{|x|}{c}\right|\leq 1
, then we obtain (
3.27
) from
Lemma

5.11
, (
2.10
) and (
3.15
).

For
t-\frac{|x|}{c}\geq 1
, recall (
2.59
). Similarly as (
3.26
), we have

\displaystyle(t-\frac{|x|}{c})\left[\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[Q_{c}(f,f)](t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|+|\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\partial_{x_{i}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[Q_{c}(f,f)](t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\right]

(3.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\gamma|=1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)Z^{\gamma}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[Q_{c}(f,f)](t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|,

and using that
Z=\hat{\Omega}_{i}-\frac{v_{0}}{c}\partial_{v_{j}}
, if
Z=\Omega_{i}
and
Z=\hat{\Omega}_{ij}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}+v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}
if
Z=\Omega_{ij}

and integration by parts in
v
for
\partial_{v_{i}}
, we obtain (
3.27
) from (
2.10
) and
3.8
.
∎

4
Improvement of the bootstrap assumptions on the electromagnetic field

In this section, we conclude the proof of
Equation

1.7
by improving the estimates for the electromagnetic fields
E
and
B
. This is achieved through a detailed analysis of the Glassey-Strauss decomposition. In the following of the section we will denote
\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}
.

4.1
Solutions of the wave equation

For any smooth function
f(t,x)
, we define that

\Phi(f)(t,x):=\frac{1}{4\pi c^{2}t^{2}}\int_{|y-x|=ct}f(0,y)\textnormal{d}y+\frac{1}{4\pi ct}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}\cdot\nabla_{y}f(0,y)\textnormal{d}y+\frac{1}{4\pi c^{2}t}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\partial_{t}f(0,y)\textnormal{d}y.

(4.1)

We can see that
\Phi(f)
only depends on the initial data of
f
.
We first give a result the wave equation.

Lemma 4.1
.

1.

For any smooth function
f
, the function
\Phi(f)(t,x)
defined in (
4.1
) is the solution to the wave equation

\partial_{t}^{2}f-c^{2}\Delta_{x}f=0.

2.

For any smooth functions
f,g
, the solution to

\partial_{t}^{2}f-c^{2}\Delta_{x}f=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\hat{v}_{i}\partial_{t}+c^{2}\partial_{x_{i}})g(t,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\quad f(0,x)=0,\quad\partial_{t}f(0,x)=0,\quad\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\quad i=1,2,3

satisfies
f(t,x)=\Phi(f)(t,x)+f_{i,1,g}(t,x)+f_{i,2,g}(t,x)+f_{i,3,g}(t,x)
, where

\displaystyle f_{i,1,g}(t,x)=-\frac{1}{4\pi c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i}}{1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v}}(T_{0}g)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y,

\displaystyle f_{i,2,g}(t,x)=-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i})}{|y-x|^{2}(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}g(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y,

\displaystyle f_{i,3,g}(t,x)=-\frac{1}{4\pi ct}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\omega_{i}-\frac{c^{-1}(\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i})(\hat{v}\cdot\omega)}{1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v}}\right)g(0,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y.

Moreover, for the
x
derivative of
f_{i,2,g}(t,x)
we have that

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,g}(t,x)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i})c^{-1}\omega_{k}}{|y-x|^{2}(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{3}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}(T_{0}g)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi c^{2}t^{2}}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i})\omega_{k}}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{3}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}g(0,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y:=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,j,g}(t,x),

with

a(\omega,v)=\frac{3(\omega_{i}+c^{-1}\hat{v}_{i})(-c^{-1}\hat{v}_{k}(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})+(c^{-2}|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)\omega_{k})+(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{2}\delta_{ik}}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{4}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}.

3.

Similarly, the solution to

\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{f}-c^{2}\Delta_{x}\tilde{f}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}c(\hat{v}_{i}\partial_{x_{j}}-\hat{v}_{j}\partial_{x_{i}})g(t,x,v)dv,\quad\tilde{f}(0,x)=0,\quad\partial_{t}\tilde{f}(0,x)=0,\quad\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\quad{i=1,2,3}

satisfies
\tilde{f}(t,x)=\Phi(f)(t,x)+\tilde{f}_{i,j,1,g}(t,x)+\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,g}(t,x)+\tilde{f}_{i,j,3,g}(t,x)
, where

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{i,j,1,g}(t,x)=\frac{1}{4\pi c^{2}}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\hat{v}_{i}\omega_{j}-\hat{v}_{j}\omega_{i}}{1+c^{-1}\hat{v}\cdot\omega}(T_{0}g)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y,

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,g}(t,x)=\frac{1}{4\pi c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\hat{v}_{i}\omega_{j}-\hat{v}_{j}\omega_{i})}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}g(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y,

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{i,j,3,g}(t,x)=\frac{1}{4\pi c^{2}t}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\hat{v}_{i}\omega_{j}-\hat{v}_{j}\omega_{i}}{(1+c^{-1}\hat{v}\cdot\omega)}g(0,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y.

Moreover, for the derivative of
\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,g}(t,x)
we have that

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,g}(t,x)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\hat{v}_{i}\omega_{j}-\hat{v}_{j}\omega_{i})c^{-1}\omega_{k}}{|y-x|^{2}(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{3}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}(T_{0}g)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\pi c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}b(\omega,v)f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\pi c^{3}t^{2}}\int_{|y-x|=ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\hat{v}_{i}\omega_{j}-\hat{v}_{j}\omega_{i})\omega_{k}}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{3}}\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}g(0,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y:=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,n,g}(t,x),

with
b(\omega,v)
satisfies

b(\omega,v)=\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}\frac{(\delta_{ik}\hat{v}_{j}-\delta_{jk}\hat{v}_{i})}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{2}}+\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}^{2}}[\frac{(\omega_{i}\hat{v}_{j}-\omega_{j}\hat{v}_{i})[(-3+3c^{-2}|\hat{v}|^{2})\omega_{k}-3c^{-1}\hat{v}_{k}(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})]}{(1+c^{-1}\omega\cdot\hat{v})^{4}}.

Moreover, for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
we have that
\int_{|\omega|=1}a(\omega,v)\textnormal{d}\omega=\int_{|\omega|=1}b(\omega,v)\textnormal{d}\omega=0
.

Proof.

(1) is a classical result can be found in any classical textbook. For (2) and (3), the proof for the case
c=1
can be found in
[
19
]
, see also
[
22
,
45
]
. The proof for general
c\geq 1
is similar (the main difference is to replace
\hat{v}
in
[
19
]
by
c^{-1}\hat{v}
in our proof), we omit the proof here. We refer to the second author’s homepage for completeness.
∎

With this equality, before we actually give the proof, we first give the derivative formula.

Lemma 4.2
.

For any smooth functions
f(\omega,v),g(t,x),h(x)
and
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{3},c\geq 1,t\geq 0
, we have

\partial_{x_{i}}\int_{|y-x|=ct}f(\omega,v)h(y)\textnormal{d}y=\int_{|y-x|=ct}f(\omega,v)(\partial_{x_{i}}h)(y)\textnormal{d}y,

(4.2)

\partial_{x_{i}}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{k}}f(\omega,v)g(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\textnormal{d}y=\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{k}}f(\omega,v)(\partial_{x_{i}}g)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\textnormal{d}y,

(4.3)

and (
4.3
) also applies for the integral on
|y-x|=ct
.

Proof.

Using the change of variable
z=y-x
we have that

\int_{|y-x|=ct}f(\omega,v)h(y)\textnormal{d}y=\int_{|z|\leq ct}f(\frac{z}{|z|},v)h(z+x)\textnormal{d}z,

and

\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{f(\omega,v)}{|y-x|^{k}}g(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)dy=\int_{|z|\leq ct}\frac{f(\frac{z}{|z|},v)}{|z|^{k}}g(t-\frac{1}{c}z,z+x)\textnormal{d}z,

which implies that

\textnormal{l.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{derivative for y - x = c t}=\int_{|z|=ct}f(\frac{z}{|z|},v)\partial_{x_{i}}h(z+x)\textnormal{d}z,=\textnormal{r.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{derivative for y - x = c t},

and

\displaystyle\textnormal{l.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{derivative for y - x le c t}=

\displaystyle\int_{|z|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|z|^{k}}f(\frac{z}{|z|},v)\partial_{x_{i}}g(t-\frac{1}{c}z,z+x)\textnormal{d}z=\textnormal{r.h.s. of}\,\,\eqref{derivative for y - x le c t},

and (
4.3
) applies similaly for the integral on
|y-x|=ct
. so the proof is thus finished.
∎

4.2
Estimates of electromagnetic field

In this subsection we improve Assumptions
BA1
,
BA2
. First by Lemma
2.18
and Lemma
4.1
, for
E,B
we have the followng lemma.

Lemma 4.3
.

For any
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, for
l=1,2,...,6
we have that

\left(Z^{\alpha}[E,B]\right)_{l}=\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\sum_{i,j,n=1}^{3}C_{\alpha,\beta,1}f_{i,n,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}+C_{\alpha,\beta,2}\tilde{f}_{i,j,l,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}+\Phi(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[E,B]_{l}),

(4.4)

for some uniformly bounded constants
C_{\alpha,\beta,1},C_{\alpha,\beta,1}
depends also on
i,j,k,l
. And we have that

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{x_{k}}Z^{\alpha}[E,B]\right)_{l}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\sum_{n=1,3}C_{\alpha,\beta,1}(f_{i,n,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f})+C_{\alpha,\beta,2}(\tilde{f}_{i,j,n,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f})+\Phi(\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[E,B]_{l})

(4.5)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}C_{\alpha,\beta,1}\partial_{x_{k}}(f_{i,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f})+C_{\alpha,\beta,2}\partial_{x_{k}}(\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}).

Proof.

From classical theory for the wave equation, the solution to

\partial_{t}^{2}f-c^{2}\Delta_{x}f=\sum_{i=1}^{N}g_{i}(t,x),\quad f(0,x)=h(x),\quad\partial_{t}f(0,x)=g(x),\quad\forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

satisfies
f=f_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{N}f_{i}
, where
f_{0}=\Phi(f)
, and
f_{i}
satisfies

\partial_{t}^{2}f_{i}-c^{2}\Delta_{x}f_{i}=g_{i}(t,x),\quad f_{i}(0,x)=\partial_{t}f_{i}(0,x)=0.

Using
Equation

2.41
, together with
Lemma

4.1
and
Lemma

4.2
, we obtain (
4.4
) and (
4.5
).
∎

Now we are ready to improve Assumption
BA1
.

Lemma 4.4
.

Under the assumptions
BA1
to
BA3
and the initial data assumption (
1.6
) and (
3.7
), there exists a universal constant
C_{2}
, such that

|Z^{\alpha}[E,B]|(t,x)\leq\frac{C_{2}(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}+M)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}\quad\forall|\alpha|\leq N-1.

(4.6)

Proof.

First, the term
\left|\frac{1}{1+c^{-1}v\cdot\omega}\right|\leq\frac{1}{1-\frac{|v|}{v_{0}}}=\frac{v_{0}}{v_{0}-|v|}\leq\frac{2v_{0}^{2}}{c^{2}}\leq\langle v\rangle^{2}
is uniformly bounded from above. According to Lemma
4.3
, it suffices to estimate
f_{i,n,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
,
\tilde{f}_{i,j,n,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
, and
\Phi(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[E,B]_{l})
for
|\beta|\leq N-1
and
i,n\in\{1,2,3\},l=1,2,...,6
. Regarding
f_{i,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and
\tilde{f}_{i,j,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
, recall from (
2.64
) that
v_{0}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}f)
contains terms of the form
\hat{Z}^{\beta}(v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f))
(the Boltzmann collision term) and
Z^{\beta_{1}}[E,B]_{k}
times
\partial_{v}(\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f)
, where
\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta
. By applying integration by parts with respect to
v
and employing Lemma
2.14
, Assumption
BA1
, Lemma
3.11
, and Corollary
3.13
, we establish the existence of a function
\Psi(\omega,v)
(maybe different in different lines) satisfying (
3.22
) such that:

\displaystyle|f_{i,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\tilde{f}_{i,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|

(4.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)(T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f))(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\left|\frac{1}{c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|}|Z^{\beta_{1}}[E,B]|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{v}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{c}\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{30}\left|\frac{1}{v_{0}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]\right|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y|)^{3_{+}}(1+\left|t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|-\frac{1}{c}|y|\right|)}\frac{1}{|x-y|}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{1_{+}}}.

For
f_{i,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and
\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
term, recall the estimate of
\hat{Z}^{\beta}f
. From Assumption (
BA3
). By Lemma
2.15
and Lemma
3.11
, we have

\displaystyle|f_{i,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y|)^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-y|^{2}}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}.

(4.8)

For
f_{i,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
,
\tilde{f}_{i,j,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and
\Phi(\hat{Z}^{\beta}[E,B]_{i})
term, recall the requirement (
1.6
) of
(f_{0},E_{0},B_{0})
. From
Lemma

2.7
, we have

\displaystyle|f_{i,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\tilde{f}_{i,j,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\Phi(\hat{Z}^{\beta}[E,B]_{l})(t,x)|

(4.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{|\gamma|\leq N+1}\|\langle x\rangle^{100}\partial_{x}^{\gamma}[E_{0},B_{0}](x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+1}\|\langle x\rangle^{100}\langle v\rangle^{100}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\partial_{v}^{\beta}f_{0}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\times\max\left\{\frac{1}{c^{2}t^{2}},\frac{1}{ct}\right\}\times\int_{|x-y|=ct}(1+y)^{-10}\textnormal{d}S_{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}+M)\max\left\{\frac{1}{c^{2}t^{2}},\frac{1}{ct}\right\}\min\{ct,c^{2}t^{2}\}(1+ct+|x|)^{-1}(1+|ct-|x||)^{-10}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}+M)(1+ct+|x|)^{-1}(1+|ct-|x||)^{-10},

and
Equation

4.6
is thus proved by combining (
4.7
) to (

) together.
∎

We then improve Assumption
BA2
.

Lemma 4.5
.

Let
\epsilon_{0}\lesssim\epsilon^{2}
, where

is given in (
1.6
). Under the assumptions
BA1
to
BA3
, for any
