|\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}Z^{\alpha}[E,B]|(t,x)+|\nabla_{x}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]|(t,x)\leq\frac{C_{3}(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}^{2}e^{DM_{1}}+M)\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}},\quad\forall|\alpha|\leq N-1.

(4.10)

Proof.

We first prove (
4.10
) for
\nabla_{x}Z^{\gamma}[E,B]
term. From Lemma
4.3
we only need to estimate

f_{i,n,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f},\quad\tilde{f}_{i,j,n,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f},\quad\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f},\quad\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f},\quad(\Phi(\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[E,B]))_{l},\quad|\beta|\leq N-1,\quad i,j=1,2,3,\quad n=1,3.

For
f_{i,1,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and
\tilde{f}_{i,j,1,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
, we follow the analysis similar as (
4.7
). Using (
2.64
), together with integration by parts in
v
and then taking
\partial_{x_{k}}
derivative. Then since
|\beta|\leq N-1
, using Assumption
BA2
, Lemma
3.12
and Corollary
3.13
, and Lemma
2.14
, there exists
\Psi(\omega,v)
(maybe different in different lines) that satisfies (
3.22
), such that

\displaystyle|f_{i,1,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\tilde{f}_{i,j,1,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|

(4.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{c}\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)(\partial_{x_{k}}T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f))(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\left|\frac{1}{c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|}|\partial_{x_{k}}Z^{\beta_{1}}[E,B]|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{v}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\left|\frac{1}{c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|}|Z^{\beta_{1}}[E,B]|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\partial_{x_{k}}(\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{c}\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{v}\Psi(\omega,v)\frac{1}{v_{0}}\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)](t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{c}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y|)^{4}(1+\left|t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|-\frac{1}{c}|y|\right|)}\frac{1}{|x-y|}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log(3+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}.

For the term
\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,3}
and
\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,3}
, using (
4.2
), similarly as (
4.9
), we have that

\displaystyle|f_{i,3,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\tilde{f}_{i,j,3,\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}(1+ct+|x|)^{-1}(1+|ct-|x||)^{-10}.

(4.12)

Finally we come to the term
\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
,
\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and and
\Phi(\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[E,B]_{l})
. Recall Lemma
4.1
, they are decomposed into 3 parts. For the last part
\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
and
\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
, the proof is the same as before, we have that

\displaystyle|\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,3,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\Phi(\partial_{x_{k}}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}[E,B]_{l})|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}+M)(1+ct+|x|)^{-1}(1+|ct-|x||)^{-10}.

(4.13)

For the term
\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,1}
and
\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,2,1}
, similar as (
4.7
), using Lemma
2.15
, , Assumption
BA1
Lemma
3.11
and Corollary
3.13
we have that

\displaystyle|\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|+|\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,1,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)|

(4.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)(T_{0}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f))(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq|\beta|}\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}|Z^{\beta_{1}}[E,B]|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y)\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\hat{Z}^{\beta_{2}}f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{30}\left|\frac{1}{v_{0}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}[v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f)]\right|(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}^{2}e^{DM_{1}}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y|)^{4}}\frac{1}{|x-y|^{2}}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}^{2}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)^{2}}.

For the term
\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{k}}f_{i,2,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}(t,x)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\pi}\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}+\int_{1\leq|y-x|\leq ct}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y:=T_{1}+T_{2}.

For the
T_{2}
term, using Lemma
Lemma

3.11
and Lemma
2.16
we have that

|T_{2}|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\int_{1\leq|y-x|\leq ct}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y|)^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-y|^{3}}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}(1+|t-\frac{|x|}{c}|)}.

(4.15)

For the
T_{1}
term, recall
\int_{|\omega|=1}a(\omega,v)\textnormal{d}\omega=0
, we have

\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,x,v)\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y=0.

Thus

\displaystyle T_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}[(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y,v)-(\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,x,v)]\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a(\omega,v)\frac{1}{|y-x|^{3}}[(y-x)\cdot(\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f)(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y+s(x-y),v)]\textnormal{d}s\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}y.

Since
|y-x|\leq 1
we have that
1+t\lesssim 1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|
, and for all
t^{\prime}\in[0,1]
, we have that
1+|y+t^{\prime}(x-y)|\geq 1+\min\{|y|,|x|\}\geq|x|
, which together with Lemma
3.12
implies that

\displaystyle T_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi(\omega,v)\nabla_{x}\hat{Z}^{\beta}f(t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|,y+s(x-y),v)\textnormal{d}v\right|\textnormal{d}y\textnormal{d}s

(4.16)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\frac{1}{(1+t-\frac{1}{c}|y-x|+|y+s(x-y)|)^{3}}\textnormal{d}y\textnormal{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\int_{|y-x|\leq 1}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}},

so we have finish the proofs by gathering (
4.11
) to (
4.16
), as the
\partial_{x_{k}}\tilde{f}_{i,j,2,2,\hat{Z}^{\beta}f}
term can be proved similarly. The proof for
\frac{1}{c}\partial_{t}
follows from the fact that

\partial_{t}E=c\nabla\times B-J,\quad\partial_{t}B=-c\nabla\times E,

together with Lemma
3.11
and Lemma
2.17
.
∎

Now we are ready to prove
Theorem

1.1
.

Proof of
Theorem

1.1
.

For any given
M>0
, let
D,C_{1},C_{2},C_{3},C_{4}
be the universal constants in (
3.7
), (
4.6
), (
4.10
) and (
3.23
) denote
C_{5}=\max\{C_{1},C_{2},C_{3},C_{4}\}
. Now we choose

M_{1}=4C_{5}M,\quad\epsilon_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{1}{16C_{5}^{2}e^{2DM_{1}}},\frac{1}{16C_{5}M_{1}^{2}e^{DM_{1}}}\right\},

then we have for any
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,\epsilon_{0}]
,

C_{1}\sqrt{\epsilon_{1}}e^{DM_{1}}\leq\frac{1}{2},\quad C_{2}(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}+M)+C_{3}(\epsilon_{1}M_{1}^{2}e^{DM_{1}}+M)+C_{4}\epsilon_{1}M_{1}e^{DM_{1}}\leq\frac{1}{2}M_{1},

and we conclude that the bootstrap assumptions (
BA1
)–(
BA3
) are strictly improved. Through a standard continuity argument, this allows for the further extension of the solution’s lifespan, thereby implying that
T_{\textnormal{max}}=+\infty
.

Furthermore, we recall that the terms
f_{i}
and
\tilde{f}_{i}
in the representation formula (
4.1
) correspond to the electric field
E(t,x)
and the magnetic field
B(t,x)
, respectively. Based on the structural properties of
f_{i}
and
\tilde{f}_{i}
, the estimates for
B(t,x)
are found to be precisely
1/c
times those obtained for
E(t,x)
. Consequently, the global bounds (
1.7
) and (
1.8
) follow directly from the bootstrap estimates (
BA1
) and (
BA3
). This completes the proof of
Theorem

1.1
.
∎

5
The relativistic Boltzmann operator

This section focuses on the relativistic Boltzmann operator. We provide its core estimates and establish a rigorous proof of the associated chain rule. We recall that the relativistic Boltzmann collision operator
Q_{c}
writes

Q_{c}(h,f)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}B_{c}(v,u)\left(h(u^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})-h(u)f(v)\right)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u,

(5.1)

where

g=\sqrt{2(v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot w-c^{2})},\quad s=2(v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot w+c^{2}),\quad s=g^{2}+4c^{2},

and

B_{c}(v,w)=v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta),\quad v_{\phi}=\frac{cg\sqrt{s}}{4v_{0}u_{0}},\quad\sigma(g,\vartheta)\vcentcolon=|g|^{\gamma-1}\sigma_{0}(\vartheta),\quad 0\leq\sigma_{0}(\vartheta)\lesssim 1,

and the scattering angle
\vartheta
is defined as

\cos\vartheta=\frac{-(v_{0}-u_{0})(v_{0}^{\prime}-u_{0}^{\prime})+(v-u)(v^{\prime}-u^{\prime})}{g^{2}},

(5.2)

v^{\prime}
and
u^{\prime}

stand for the postcollisional momenta of particles which had the momenta
v
and
u
before the collision, respectively. In the center-of-momentum frame, under the scattering angle
\omega
, we have

\displaystyle v^{\prime}=\frac{v+u}{2}+\frac{g}{2}\left(\omega+(\zeta-1)(v+u)\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{|v+u|^{2}}\right),\quad u^{\prime}=\frac{v+u}{2}-\frac{g}{2}\left(\omega+(\zeta-1)(v+u)\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{|v+u|^{2}}\right),

where
\zeta=\frac{u_{0}+v_{0}}{\sqrt{s}}
. We compute that

\zeta-1=\frac{v_{0}+u_{0}-\sqrt{s}}{\sqrt{s}}=\frac{(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}-s}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}=\frac{v_{0}^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+2v_{0}u_{0}-s}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}=\frac{|v+u|^{2}}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})},

thus we also have that

\displaystyle v^{\prime}=\frac{v+u}{2}+\frac{g}{2}\left(\omega+(v+u)\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}\right),\quad u^{\prime}=\frac{v+u}{2}-\frac{g}{2}\left(\omega+(v+u)\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}\right),

(5.3)

and we can also compute that

v_{0}^{\prime}=\frac{v_{0}+u_{0}}{2}+\frac{g}{2\sqrt{s}}\omega\cdot(v+u),\quad u_{0}^{\prime}=\frac{v_{0}+u_{0}}{2}-\frac{g}{2\sqrt{s}}\omega\cdot(v+u).

(5.4)

5.1
Basic estimates

Let us start by providing some basic estimates in the relativistic regime.

Lemma 5.1
.

We have

c\frac{|v-u|}{\sqrt{v_{0}u_{0}}}\leq g\leq|v-u|,\quad g\leq 5\min\left\{\frac{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle\sqrt{u_{0}}}{\sqrt{v_{0}}},\frac{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle\sqrt{v_{0}}}{\sqrt{u_{0}}}\right\},\quad 4c^{2}\leq s\leq 4v_{0}u_{0},

(5.5)

moreover, for any
\gamma\in(-2,0]
, we have that

|v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)|\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}.

(5.6)

Proof.

The inequalities in (
5.5
) are proved in Lemma 2.3 of
[
7
]
. We next prove (
5.6
). Using that
\sqrt{s}\lesssim g+c,g\leq\sqrt{v_{0}u_{0}}
and (
5.5
), for
\gamma\in[-1,0]
, we have that

v_{\phi}|\sigma(g,\vartheta)|\lesssim v_{\phi}|g|^{\gamma-1}=\frac{cg\sqrt{s}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma-1}\lesssim\frac{c^{2}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma}+\frac{c}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma+1}\lesssim\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}u_{0}}\left(\frac{v_{0}u_{0}}{c^{2}}\right)^{\frac{-\gamma}{2}}\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}+\frac{c}{\sqrt{v_{0}u_{0}}}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}},

then if
\gamma+1\in(-1,0]
, using that
a^{\gamma+1}\lesssim a^{\gamma}+1
we have that

v_{\phi}|\sigma(g,\vartheta)|\lesssim v_{\phi}|g|^{\gamma-1}=\frac{cg\sqrt{s}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma-1}=\frac{c\sqrt{s}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma}\lesssim\frac{c^{2}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma}+\frac{c}{4v_{0}u_{0}}\lesssim\frac{c^{2}}{v_{0}u_{0}}\left(\frac{v_{0}u_{0}}{c^{2}}\right)^{\frac{-\gamma}{2}}\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}+1\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}},

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

Suppose that
v^{\prime},u^{\prime}
are defined before, then for any
c\geq 1
we have

1+|v^{\prime}|^{2}\leq 1+3(|v|^{2}+|u|^{2}),\quad 1+|u^{\prime}|^{2}\leq 1+3(|v|^{2}+|u|^{2}).

(5.7)

Proof.

Remind (
5.3
). Together with
u_{0}+v_{0}\geq|v+u|
and (
5.5
) and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have

|v^{\prime}|^{2}\leq 3\left(\frac{|v+u|^{2}}{4}+\frac{g^{2}}{4}+\frac{g^{2}|v+u|^{2}}{4s}\right)\leq 3\left(\frac{|v+u|^{2}}{4}+\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{4}+\frac{|v+u|^{2}}{4}\right)\leq 3(|v|^{2}+|u|^{2}),

the proof of
u^{\prime}
is similar.
∎

Lemma 5.3
.

For the
v,u,v^{\prime}
defined before we have that

\min\left\{\left|\frac{v^{\prime}}{v_{0}^{\prime}}-\frac{v}{v_{0}}\right|,\left|\frac{v^{\prime}}{v_{0}^{\prime}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|\right\}\leq C\min\left\{\frac{v_{0}^{2}}{c^{2}},\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{c^{2}}\right\}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|\leq C\min\{\langle v\rangle^{2},\langle u\rangle^{2}\}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|,

(5.8)

for some uniform constant
C>0
that does not depend on
v,w,\omega,c
. By reverse the order of
v,u
and
v^{\prime},u^{\prime}
we have that

\min\left\{\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u^{\prime}}{u_{0}^{\prime}}\right|,\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{v^{\prime}}{v_{0}^{\prime}}\right|\right\}\leq C\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2}\}\left|\frac{v^{\prime}}{v_{0}^{\prime}}-\frac{u^{\prime}}{u_{0}^{\prime}}\right|.

Moreover, there exists a constant
C
does not depend on
v,w,\omega,c,x,t
, such that for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},t\geq 0
,

\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle\leq C\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2}\}(\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle+\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle).

(5.9)

Proof.

Without losing generality, we assume that
|v|\geq|u|
, i.e.,
v_{0}\geq u_{0}
, the case
|v|\leq|u|
can be proved by switching
u
and
v
. First, we have

\displaystyle v_{0}u_{0}g^{2}-|vu_{0}-uv_{0}|^{2}=

\displaystyle v_{0}u_{0}(2v_{0}u_{0}-2v\cdot u-2c^{2})-|v|^{2}u_{0}^{2}+2v\cdot uv_{0}u_{0}-|u|^{2}v_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2v_{0}^{2}u_{0}^{2}-|v|^{2}u_{0}^{2}-|u|^{2}v_{0}^{2}-2c^{2}v_{0}u_{0}=c^{2}u_{0}^{2}+c^{2}v_{0}^{2}-2c^{2}v_{0}u_{0}=c^{2}(v_{0}-u_{0})^{2}.

Recall that
g\geq\frac{c|v-u|}{\sqrt{v_{0}u_{0}}}
, we have that

|vu_{0}-uv_{0}|^{2}=v_{0}u_{0}g^{2}-c^{2}(v_{0}-u_{0})^{2}\geq c^{2}|v-u|^{2}-c^{2}(v_{0}-u_{0})^{2}=c^{2}g^{2},

which means that

g\lesssim\frac{\left|uv_{0}-vu_{0}\right|}{c}\lesssim\frac{v_{0}u_{0}}{c}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|,\quad g\lesssim\frac{\left|uv_{0}-vu_{0}\right|+c|u_{0}-v_{0}|}{\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}}=\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|+\frac{c|u_{0}-v_{0}|}{\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}}.

(5.10)

Recall (
5.3
) and (
5.4
), we compute

\displaystyle v^{\prime}v_{0}-vv_{0}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\frac{(v+u)v_{0}}{2}+\frac{v_{0}g}{2}\left(\omega+(v+u)\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}\right)-\frac{v(v_{0}+u_{0})}{2}-\frac{vg}{2\sqrt{s}}\omega\cdot(v+u)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{uv_{0}-vu_{0}}{2}+\frac{v_{0}g}{2}\omega+\frac{g(v+u)\cdot\omega}{2\sqrt{s}}\left(\frac{(v+u)v_{0}}{v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s}}-v\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{uv_{0}-vu_{0}}{2}+\frac{v_{0}g}{2}\omega+\frac{g(v+u)\cdot\omega}{2\sqrt{s}}\left(\frac{uv_{0}-vu_{0}}{v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s}}-\frac{v\sqrt{s}}{v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s}}\right)\vcentcolon=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}.

From

\left|\frac{uv_{0}-vu_{0}}{2}\right|\lesssim|uv_{0}-vu_{0}|,\quad\left|\frac{g(v+u)\cdot\omega}{2\sqrt{s}}\frac{uv_{0}-vu_{0}}{v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s}}\right|\lesssim|uv_{0}-vu_{0}|\frac{(|v|+|u|)g}{\sqrt{s}(v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s})}\lesssim|uv_{0}-vu_{0}|,

we obtain that

\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}\left(\left|T_{1}\right|+\left|T_{3}\right|\right)\lesssim\frac{1}{cv_{0}}|uv_{0}-vu_{0}|=\frac{u_{0}}{c}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{u^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}},\frac{v^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}}\right\}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

(5.11)

Next, to estimate
T_{2}
and
T_{4}
, we divide into two parts.

1.

If
v_{0}\leq 100u_{0}
. Then we have

\left|T_{2}\right|\lesssim v_{0}g,\quad\left|T_{4}\right|\lesssim g|v|\frac{|u|+|v|}{u_{0}+v_{0}}\lesssim v_{0}g,

thus together with
v_{0}\sim u_{0}
, we have

\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}\left(\left|T_{2}\right|+\left|T_{4}\right|\right)\lesssim\frac{v_{0}g}{cv_{0}}\lesssim\frac{u_{0}v_{0}}{c^{2}}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{u^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}},\frac{v^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}}\right\}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

(5.12)

2.

If
v_{0}\geq 100u_{0}
. If
g\leq\frac{\sqrt{s}}{2}
, we have

v_{0}^{\prime}=\frac{u_{0}+v_{0}}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{g}}{2\sqrt{s}}\omega\cdot(u+v)\geq\frac{u_{0}+v_{0}}{2}-\frac{g}{2\sqrt{s}}\left|u+v\right|\geq\frac{u_{0}+v_{0}}{4},

and

\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}\left(\left|T_{2}\right|+\left|T_{4}\right|\right)\lesssim\frac{v_{0}g}{v^{2}_{0}}\lesssim\frac{u_{0}}{c}\frac{\left|uv_{0}-vu_{0}\right|}{u_{0}v_{0}}\lesssim\min\left\{\frac{u^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}},\frac{v^{2}_{0}}{c^{2}}\right\}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

(5.13)

Next, we compute

\displaystyle T_{2}+T_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{g}{2}\left(v_{0}\omega-\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{v_{0}+u_{0}+\sqrt{s}}v\right)=\frac{g}{2}\left(v_{0}\omega-\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{v_{0}+u_{0}}v+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega\sqrt{s}}{(u_{0}+v_{0}+\sqrt{s})(u_{0}+v_{0})}v\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{g}{2}\left((u_{0}+v_{0})\omega-\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{u_{0}+v_{0}}(u+v)-v_{0}\omega+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{u_{0}+v_{0}}u+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega\sqrt{s}}{(u_{0}+v_{0}+\sqrt{s})(u_{0}+v_{0})}v\right).

Remind
v_{0}\geq 100u_{0}
, we have
|v|\geq\max\{c,100|u|\}
. Thus

(vu_{0}-uv_{0})\cdot(uv_{0}+vu_{0})=v^{2}u_{0}^{2}-u^{2}v_{0}^{2}=c^{2}(|v|^{2}-|u|^{2})\geq\frac{c^{2}|v|^{2}}{2},

which implies that

\left|uv_{0}-vu_{0}\right|\geq\frac{c^{2}|v|^{2}}{2\left|uv_{0}+vu_{0}\right|}\geq\frac{c^{2}|v|^{2}}{4u_{0}v_{0}}\geq\frac{c^{2}v_{0}}{16u_{0}}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\frac{c^{2}}{u_{0}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{|uv_{0}-vu_{0}|}{u_{0}v_{0}}=\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

Together with (
5.10
), we have

g\lesssim\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|+\frac{c\sqrt{v_{0}}}{\sqrt{u_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}u_{0}}{c}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

Thus

\frac{g}{2}\left|-v_{0}\omega+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{u_{0}+v_{0}}u+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega\sqrt{s}}{(u_{0}+v_{0}+\sqrt{s})(u_{0}+v_{0})}v\right|\lesssim gu_{0}+g\sqrt{s}\lesssim g\sqrt{u_{0}v_{0}}\lesssim\frac{v_{0}u_{0}^{2}}{c}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|,

which means that

\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}\frac{g}{2}\left|-v_{0}\omega+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega}{u_{0}+v_{0}}u+\frac{(u+v)\cdot\omega\sqrt{s}}{(u_{0}+v_{0}+\sqrt{s})(u_{0}+v_{0})}v\right|\lesssim\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{c^{2}}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|.

(5.14)

Next, we compute

\displaystyle\left|(v_{0}+u_{0})\omega-\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{(v_{0}+u_{0})}(v+u)\right|^{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}}[(v_{0}+u_{0})^{4}-2(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}+|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}|v+u|^{2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}}[(v_{0}+u_{0})^{4}-(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}]\lesssim[(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}-|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}].

We also have that

v_{0}^{\prime}u_{0}^{\prime}=\frac{(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}}{4}+\frac{g^{2}}{4s}|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}\geq\frac{g^{2}}{4s}[(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}-|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}]\geq\frac{1}{16}[(v_{0}+u_{0})^{2}-|\omega\cdot(v+u)|^{2}],

and we have that
u_{0}^{\prime}\leq v_{0}+u_{0}\leq 2v_{0}
, thus we have that

\left|(v_{0}+u_{0})\omega-\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{(u_{0}+v_{0})}(v+u)\right|^{2}\lesssim v_{0}^{\prime}u_{0}^{\prime}\lesssim v_{0}v_{0}^{\prime},

which means that

\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}g\left|(v_{0}+u_{0})\omega-\frac{(v+u)\cdot\omega}{(q_{0}+p_{0})}(v+u)\right|\lesssim g\frac{1}{\sqrt{v_{0}^{\prime}v_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{u_{0}}u_{0}}{c^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left|\frac{v}{v_{0}}-\frac{u}{u_{0}}\right|,

(5.15)

and (
5.8
) is thus proved by combining (
5.11
), (
5.12
), (
5.14
), (
5.15
) together.
(
5.9
) is direct from the estimates

\min\{\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle-\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle-\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle\}\leq\min\{t|\hat{v}-\hat{v^{\prime}}|,t|\hat{v}-\hat{u^{\prime}}|\}\lesssim\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2}\}t|\hat{v^{\prime}}-\hat{u^{\prime}}|,

(5.16)

and

t|\hat{v^{\prime}}-\hat{u^{\prime}}|=|(x-t\hat{v^{\prime}})-(x-t\hat{u^{\prime}})|\leq\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle+\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle.

(5.17)

So the proof is thus finished.
∎

5.2
Estimates for the relativistic Boltzmann operator

In this subsection, we provide the estimates of the relativistic Boltzmann operator. We start by listing some propositions.

Lemma 5.4

(Pre-post collisional change of variable for relativistic Boltzmann operator)
.

[
[
51
]
, Corollary 5 and (23)]
For any smooth function
F
we have that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)F(v,w,v^{\prime},u^{\prime})\textnormal{d}\sigma\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}u=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)F(v^{\prime},u^{\prime},v,w)\textnormal{d}\sigma\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}u.

Lemma 5.5

(Relativistic Carleman Representation,
[
33
]
, Lemma 2.12)
.

For any smooth functions
f,h
, denote that

\bar{g}:=g(v,v^{\prime})=\sqrt{2(v_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-v\cdot v^{\prime}-c^{2})},\quad\tilde{g}:=g(u,v^{\prime})=\sqrt{2(u_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-u\cdot v^{\prime}-c^{2})},

then we have

\displaystyle Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{8\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\mathpzc{u}(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})h(v+u-v^{\prime})\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{8\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})h(v+u-v^{\prime})\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u,

where

\mathpzc{u}(x)=1,\quad\hbox{if}\quad x\geq c,\quad\mathpzc{u}(x)=0,\quad\hbox{if}\quad x<c.

Proof.

The first equality just follows from
[
33
]
, Lemma 2.11, Lemma 2.12, see also
[
34
]
Lemma 2.4 and (5.1) in
[
32
]
. We also note that

2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})-2u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})=2(v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot u-c^{2})-2(u_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-u\cdot v^{\prime}-c^{2})=g^{2}-\tilde{g}^{2},

which equals to
\bar{g}^{2}=g^{2}-\tilde{g}^{2}
(we also refer to
[
34
]
, Section 2). Thus we have

\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)=\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right),

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 5.6

( Proposition 2.7 of
[
34
]
)
.

Remind
\vartheta
defined in (
5.2
), we have
\sin\left(\frac{\vartheta}{2}\right)=\frac{\bar{g}}{g}.

Before giving the estimates for the
Q^{+}_{c},Q^{-}_{c}
term, we first give an estimate for the intermediate term.

Lemma 5.7
.

For any
\beta\in\mathbb{R}
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})\langle v+u-v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

(5.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{g}}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|.

Proof.

We first take the solar coordinates on
w
into angular coordinates and choose the z-axis
parallel to
v-v^{\prime}
, such that the angle between
u
and
v-v^{\prime}
is equal to
\theta
, i.e.
v-v^{\prime}=(0,0,|v-v^{\prime}|)
. Then we have

(v-v^{\prime})\cdot u=|u||v-v^{\prime}|\cos\theta
. Remind
g^{2}=\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}
, where
\bar{g},\tilde{g}
defined in
Lemma

5.5
, we have that

\displaystyle|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-|v+u-v^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2}=

\displaystyle v_{0}^{2}+u_{0}^{2}+v_{0}^{\prime 2}+2v_{0}u_{0}-2u_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-2v_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-v^{2}-w^{2}-u^{\prime 2}-2v\cdot w+2w\cdot v^{\prime}+2v\cdot u^{\prime}-c^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2v_{0}u_{0}-2u_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-2v_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}-2v\cdot w+2w\cdot v^{\prime}+2v\cdot u^{\prime}+2c^{2}=g^{2}-\bar{g}^{2}-\tilde{g}^{2}=0,

so we have
v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}=(v+u-v^{\prime})_{0}=\sqrt{c^{2}+|v+u-v^{\prime}|^{2}}
. Thus taking the polar coordinates of
u
,
using that

\displaystyle\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)=

\displaystyle\delta(\frac{1}{2\bar{g}}(\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+2|u||v^{\prime}-v|\cos\theta))=\frac{\bar{g}}{|u||v^{\prime}-v|}\delta(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})}{2|u||v^{\prime}-v|}+\cos\theta),

under these spherical coordinates, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})\langle v+u-v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}+\frac{-u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\frac{\bar{g}}{|u||v^{\prime}-v|}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})}{2|u||v^{\prime}-v|}+\cos\theta\right)|u|^{2}\sin\theta\textnormal{d}|u|\textnormal{d}\theta\textnormal{d}\phi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{g}}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{-1}^{1}\int_{0}^{2\pi}u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})}{2|u||v^{\prime}-v|}+\cos\theta\right)|u|\textnormal{d}|u|\textnormal{d}(-\cos\theta)\textnormal{d}\phi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{g}}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|u_{0}^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|,

the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 5.8
.

Define

\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\vcentcolon=\int_{0}^{\infty}|u||u_{0}|^{\beta}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|,

(5.19)

then for any
v,v^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
|v|\geq|v^{\prime}|
, and for any
\beta\geq-1,k\geq\max\{9,\beta+10\},c\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim c^{\beta}(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-(k-1)/2}+(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}))+c^{2})^{-(k-\beta-2)/2}\lesssim c^{\beta}.

(5.20)

Moreover if we assume further that
|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|
, for any
\beta\geq-1,k\geq 8
we have that

\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim c^{\beta}\langle v\rangle^{-(k-1)}+\langle v\rangle^{-(k-\beta-2)},

(5.21)

for any
v,v^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
|v|\leq|v^{\prime}|
, for any
k\geq 8
we have that

\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim\frac{(v_{0}^{\prime})^{\beta+1}}{c}.

(5.22)

Gathering (
5.20
) and (
5.22
), for
|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|
, for any
k\geq 8
we have that

\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim\frac{(v_{0}^{\prime})^{\beta+1}}{c}+c^{\beta},

(5.23)

where all the constants are independent of
v,v^{\prime},c,f
.

Proof.

1.

If
|v|\geq|v^{\prime}|
then we have
v_{0}\geq v_{0}^{\prime}
, let
\mathpzc v=u_{0},\mathpzc w=\mathpzc v-c
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}|u||u_{0}|^{\beta}(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\lesssim\int_{c}^{\infty}\mathpzc v^{\beta+1}(1+|v_{0}+\mathpzc v-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathpzc w+c)^{\beta+1}(1+|v_{0}+\mathpzc w+c-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathpzc w+c)^{\beta+1}(1+|v_{0}+\mathpzc w-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2\mathpzc wc+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w\vcentcolon=\mathpzc C_{1}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c).

We estimate two cases
\mathpzc w\geq c
and
\mathpzc w\leq c
seperately. For the case
\mathpzc w\leq c
, taking
y=\mathpzc wc
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathpzc C_{1}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}++2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-(k-1)/2}\int_{0}^{c}c^{\beta+1}(1+2\mathpzc wc)^{-1/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w

(5.24)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c^{\beta}(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-(k-1)/2}\int_{0}^{1}(1+2y)^{-1/2}\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c^{\beta}(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-(k-1)/2}.

For the case
\mathpzc w\geq c
, using the change of variable
\mathpzc w=\sqrt{1+|v_{0}-v^{\prime}_{0}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+c^{2}}y
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C_{1}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathpzc w^{\beta+1}(1+|v_{0}+\mathpzc w-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}))+c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w

(5.25)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathpzc w^{\beta+1}(\mathpzc w^{2}+1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+c^{2})^{-(k-\beta-2)/2}\int_{0}^{\infty}y^{\beta+1}(1+y^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}y

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+c^{2})^{-(k-\beta-2)/2},

and (
5.20
) is thus proved after (
5.24
) and (
5.25
).

2.

For
2|v^{\prime}|\leq|v|
we have
v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}=\frac{|v|^{2}-|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{v_{0}^{\prime}+v_{0}}\geq\frac{|v|^{2}-|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{2v_{0}}\geq\frac{|v|^{2}}{8v_{0}}
.
Moreover, we have
2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})\geq\frac{c|v|^{2}}{4v_{0}}\geq\frac{|v|^{2}}{8}
for
|v|\leq c
and
|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}\geq\frac{|v|^{4}}{64v_{0}^{2}}\geq\frac{|v|^{2}}{128}
for
|v|\geq c
. Gathering the cases above, we have

(1+|v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}+2c(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})))^{-1}\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{-2},\quad\forall|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|,\quad v,v^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

and (
5.21
) is thus proved.

3.

If
|v|\leq|v^{\prime}|
, then we have
v_{0}\leq v_{0}^{\prime}
, let
\mathpzc v=u_{0},\mathpzc w=\mathpzc v-(c+v_{0}^{\prime}-v_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}\geq c}|u||u_{0}|^{\beta}(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{v_{0}^{\prime}+c-v_{0}}^{\infty}\mathpzc v^{\beta+1}(1+|v_{0}+\mathpzc v-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathpzc w+c+v_{0}^{\prime}-v_{0})^{\beta+1}(1+\mathpzc w^{2}+2\mathpzc wc)^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w\vcentcolon=\mathpzc C_{2}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c).

If
\mathpzc w\leq c+v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C_{2}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}(c+v_{0}^{\prime}-v_{0})^{\beta+1}(1+2\mathpzc wc)^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w\lesssim\frac{(v_{0}^{\prime})^{\beta+1}}{c}\int_{0}^{\infty}(1+2y)^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}y\lesssim\frac{(v_{0}^{\prime})^{\beta+1}}{c}.

(5.26)

iIf
\mathpzc w\geq c+v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathpzc C_{2}(v,v^{\prime},\beta,k,c)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathpzc w^{\beta+1}(1+\mathpzc w^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}\mathpzc w\lesssim 1,

(5.27)

and (
5.22
) is thus proved after combining (
5.26
) and (
5.27
).

∎

Lemma 5.9
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-2,0]
, then for any
p>\frac{3}{3+\gamma},k\geq 10
and any smooth functions
h,f
we have that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}|Q_{c}(h,f)|\textnormal{d}v\lesssim(\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+(\|f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}.

Proof.

For
Q^{-}_{c}
, we use (
5.6
) and Lemma
2.6
to obtain that

\displaystyle|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}|Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)|\textnormal{d}v|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)|h(u)|\langle v\rangle^{k}|f(v)|\textnormal{d}u\textnormal{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}\right)|h(u)||f(v)|\langle v\rangle^{k}\textnormal{d}u\textnormal{d}v\lesssim(\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}.

For
Q^{+}_{c}
term, using the pre-post collisional change of variable Lemma
5.4
and Lemma
2.6
we have that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}|Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\textnormal{d}v\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\langle v\rangle^{k}|f(v^{\prime})||h(u^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}\sigma\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}u\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}|f(v)||h(u)|\textnormal{d}\sigma\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}\right)(\langle v\rangle^{k}+\langle w\rangle^{k})|f(v)||h(u)|\textnormal{d}\sigma\textnormal{d}v\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+(\|f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{1}_{v}},

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

As a direct result, we obtain that

Corollary 5.10
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-2,0]
, then for any
p>\frac{3}{3+\gamma},k\geq 10
and any smooth function
f
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}|Q_{c}(f,f)|\textnormal{d}v\lesssim(1+t)^{-3-\frac{3}{p}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k+5}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Proof.

From
Lemma

2.5
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}|Q_{c}(f,f)|\textnormal{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+t)^{-3}\|\langle v\rangle^{k+5}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\cdot(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{p}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle=(1+t)^{-3-\frac{3}{p}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k+5}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},

and the proof of the corollary is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 5.11
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-2,0]
, then for any smooth function
h,f
and
p>\frac{9}{5+\gamma}
, for any
k\geq 20
, we have that

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}},

also by reverse
v^{\prime}
,
u^{\prime}
(by the change of variable
\omega=-\omega
) we have that

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{\infty}}.

For the
Q^{-}_{c}
term we have that

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim(\|\langle v\rangle h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Proof.

The case
|v|\leq 1
is obvious, thus we can suppose
|v|\geq 1
. Using (
5.6
) and Lemma
2.6
, for any
p>\frac{3}{3+\gamma}
, we have that

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{k}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{\phi}|\sigma(g,\vartheta)||h(u)||f(v)|\textnormal{d}u\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(1+\frac{1}{|p-q|^{-\gamma}}\right)|h(u)|\textnormal{d}u\|\langle v\rangle^{k}\ f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim(\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}\ f\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Next we come to compute
Q^{+}_{c}
. For simplicity, define

\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\vcentcolon=\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})\langle v+u-v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right).

Using that
s=g^{2}+4c^{2}
, taking the
L^{\infty}
norm of
h
and using Lemma
5.5
we have that

\displaystyle|\langle p\rangle^{k}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma-1}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})|h(v+u-v^{\prime})|\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}q

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{u_{0}}|g|^{\gamma+1}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{u_{0}}c^{2}|g|^{\gamma-1}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}:=T_{1}+T_{2}.

We will split it into two cases
g\geq\frac{1}{100}
and
g\leq\frac{1}{100}
.

1.

For the case
g\geq\frac{1}{100}
, we first compute
T_{2}
, by Lemma
5.7
and using (
5.21
),(
5.23
) for
|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|
and
|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|
respectively, and using (
5.5
) to
\bar{g}
(by changing
u
to
v^{\prime}
), for any
p>\frac{3}{2}
we have that

\displaystyle T_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c^{2}}{u_{0}}|g|^{-1}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u\|\langle p\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|\frac{c}{u_{0}}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|(1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}+1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\langle v\rangle^{-k+2}\bar{g}^{-1})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle p\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\left(1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}+1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\langle v\rangle^{-k+2}\frac{\sqrt{v_{0}v_{0}^{\prime}}}{c|v-v^{\prime}|}\right)\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(1+\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\right)\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}},

where we note that
\langle v\rangle\lesssim\langle v^{\prime}\rangle
if
|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|
and
\langle v\rangle\lesssim|v-v^{\prime}|
if
|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|
.

We next estimate
T_{1}
. If
\gamma+1\geq 0
, then using
|g|^{\gamma+1}\lesssim|g|\lesssim v_{0}+u_{0}
, for any
p>\frac{3}{2}
we have

\displaystyle T_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{u_{0}}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle u\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u\right)\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{{|v^{\prime}-v|}}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|\frac{1}{u_{0}}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-u_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{1}{v_{0}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-u_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})(1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}+1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\langle v\rangle^{-k+1})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}\left(\frac{v_{0}^{\prime}}{c}1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}+1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\langle v\rangle^{-k+2}\right)f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}f(v^{\prime})1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f(v^{\prime})1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}.

If
\gamma+1\leq 0
, then we have

\displaystyle T_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{u_{0}}\mathpzc G_{k}\left(v,u,v^{\prime},c\right)\textnormal{d}u\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{0}^{\infty}|u|\frac{1}{u_{0}}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})(1+|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2})^{-k/2}\textnormal{d}|u|\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{c}{v_{0}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})(1_{|v|\leq 2|v^{\prime}|}+1_{|v|\geq 2|v^{\prime}|}\langle v\rangle^{-k+1})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}.

So the case for
|g|\geq\frac{1}{100}
is proved.

2.

For the case
g\leq\frac{1}{100}
, we have that

\frac{|v-u|}{\sqrt{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle}}\leq c\frac{|v-u|}{\sqrt{v_{0}u_{0}}}\leq g\leq\frac{1}{100}
.
Since
|v|>1
, we have
|v-u|\leq\frac{1}{100}\max\{\langle v\rangle,\langle u\rangle\}\leq\frac{1}{50}\max\{|v|,|u|\},

which implies that

\min\{|v|,|u|\}\geq\frac{19}{20}\max\{|v|,|u|\}

(5.28)

remind
g^{2}=\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}
, we have
\bar{g}\leq\frac{1}{100}
, thus we also have

\min\{|v^{\prime}|,|u|\}\geq\frac{19}{20}\max\{|v^{\prime}|,|u|\},

(5.29)

(
5.28
) and (
5.29
) imply that

\min\{|u|,|v|,|v^{\prime}|\}\geq\frac{9}{10}\max\{|u|,|v|,|v^{\prime}|\}

next,

v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}-c=\frac{|v|^{2}}{v_{0}+c}-\frac{|v^{\prime}|^{2}-|u|^{2}}{v_{0}^{\prime}+u_{0}}\geq\frac{4}{5}\frac{|v|^{2}}{v_{0}^{\prime}+u_{0}^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{5}\frac{|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{v_{0}^{\prime}+u_{0}}\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{|v|^{2}}{v_{0}^{\prime}+u_{0}^{\prime}}\geq\frac{1}{5}\frac{|v|^{2}}{v_{0}}

which means that
v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}\geq\frac{1}{5}v_{0}
. So

\langle u\rangle^{2}+\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2}+\langle v\rangle^{2}\lesssim 1+(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}-c)(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}+c)=1+(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})^{2}-c^{2}.

Now we give estimates for the case
|g|\leq\frac{1}{100}
.Choosing the axis of
u
such that
v-v^{\prime}=(0,0,|v-v^{\prime}|)
, denote
u
as
u=(\mathfrak{U},u_{3}),\mathfrak{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{2},
then we have
(v-v^{\prime})\cdot u=|v-v^{\prime}|u_{3}
, and
u^{2}=\mathfrak{U}^{2}+w_{3}^{2}
,
\langle u\rangle^{-k}\leq\langle{\mathfrak{U}}\rangle^{-k}
for
k\geq 0
. Denote

v_{\parallel}=\frac{v-v^{\prime}}{|v-v^{\prime}|}(v\cdot\frac{v-v^{\prime}}{|v-v^{\prime}|}),\quad v_{\perp}=v-v_{\parallel},\quad v_{\perp}\perp v_{\parallel},\quad v_{\perp}=(\bar{v}_{\perp},0),\quad v_{\parallel}=(0,0,\bar{v}_{\parallel}),

where
\bar{v}_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
is the first two dimensional component of
v_{\perp}
, then we have that

|v-u|=|v_{\parallel}+v_{\perp}-u|=\sqrt{|\bar{v}_{\parallel}-u_{3}|^{2}+|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|^{2}}\geq|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|.

Thus under the condition
\bar{g}\leq g\leq\frac{1}{100}
, we have that

\frac{|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|}{\sqrt{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle}}\lesssim\frac{|v-u|}{\sqrt{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle}}\lesssim g
, and

|g|^{\gamma-1}=|g|^{-(1-\gamma)}\lesssim|\bar{g}|^{-\frac{1-\gamma}{3}}|\frac{|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|}{\sqrt{\langle v\rangle\langle u\rangle}}|^{-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{1-\gamma}{3}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|^{\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{3}}}\langle q\rangle^{10}.

Using that

\displaystyle\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}}{2}-\frac{u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})-u\cdot(v-v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}}\right)=

\displaystyle\delta(\frac{1}{2\bar{g}}(\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})+2u_{3}|v-v^{\prime}|))=\frac{\bar{g}}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\delta(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})}{2|v^{\prime}-v|}+u_{3}),

by Lemma
2.6
and using the same spherical coordinates as Lemma
5.7
we compute that for any
p>\frac{9}{5+\gamma}
,

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{k}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{c}{v_{0}^{\prime}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|g|^{\gamma-1}}{u_{0}}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})1_{|g|\leq\frac{1}{100}}\langle v+u-v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|g|^{\gamma-1}1_{|g|\leq\frac{1}{100}}\langle u\rangle^{-k}\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}|f(v^{\prime})|}{|v^{\prime}-v|^{1+\frac{1-\gamma}{3}}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{|\bar{v}_{\perp}-\mathfrak{U}|^{\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{3}}}\langle\mathfrak{U}\rangle^{k-10}\textnormal{d}\mathfrak{U}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\delta(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}-2u_{0}(v_{0}-u_{0}^{\prime})}{2|v^{\prime}-v|}+u_{3})\textnormal{d}u_{3}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{k}h\|_{L^{\infty}},

we then end the proof by noting that
\frac{9}{5+\gamma}>\frac{3}{2}
and
\frac{9}{5+\gamma}>\frac{3}{3+\gamma}
for
\gamma\in(-2,0]
.

∎

Theorem 5.12
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-2,0]
, then for any smooth functions
h,f
and any
t\geq 0,x\in\mathbb{R}^{3},0<\alpha<\frac{5+\gamma}{3}
for any
k\geq 10
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}Q_{c}(h,f)|+|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}Q_{c}(h,f)|

(5.30)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\alpha}\left(\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\right)\left(\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\right).

Remark 5.13
.

For
\gamma\in(-2,0]
we have that
\frac{5+\gamma}{3}>1
, which means that the convergence rate is larger than
1
.

Proof.

Denote
p=\frac{3}{\alpha}
, thus
p>\frac{9}{5+\gamma}>\frac{3}{3+\gamma}
. For
Q^{-}_{c}
term, by Lemma
2.5
and (
5.6
), we have

\displaystyle|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)|h(u)||f(v)|\textnormal{d}u\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(1+\frac{1}{|v-u|^{-\gamma}}\right)|h(u)|\textnormal{d}u\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{10}\langle v\rangle^{10}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Then we come to prove the
Q^{+}_{c}
term, by (
5.9
) we have that

\displaystyle\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}\lesssim\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}}\}(\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}}+\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}})(\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}+\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}),

thus we have that

\displaystyle|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}|h(u^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}}\}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}|h(u^{\prime})|\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\min\{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}},\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}}\}\langle x-t\hat{u^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}}|h(u^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\sigma(g,\vartheta)\langle u^{\prime}\rangle^{2k_{1}}|h(u^{\prime})|\langle x-t\hat{v^{\prime}}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k_{2}}|f(v^{\prime})|\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}|f|)+Q_{c}^{+}(\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}|h|,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}|h|)

\displaystyle+\min\{Q^{+}_{c}(\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}|h|,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}|f|),Q_{c}^{+}(\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}|f|)\}

\displaystyle+\min\{Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}|f|),Q_{c}^{+}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k_{1}}\langle v\rangle^{2k_{1}}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{k_{2}}|f|)\}.

By taking
k=10
in Lemma
5.11
we have that

|\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)|\lesssim\min\{(\|\langle v\rangle^{10}h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{10}h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{10}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},(\|\langle v\rangle^{10}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{10}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})\|\langle v\rangle^{10}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\}.

(5.31)

Now we are ready to give the estimates for the left side of (
5.30
).

1.

We have

\displaystyle|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{10}|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k}|f|)+\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h|,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}|f|)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}|f|)+\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}|f|)

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}.

From (
5.31
) and Lemma
2.5
, we have

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}(\|\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})

(5.32)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Similarly,

T_{2}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(5.33)

Next, we have

\displaystyle T_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}(\|\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})

(5.34)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Similarly,

T_{4}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(5.35)

2.

Next, we calculate

\displaystyle|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{10}|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}|f|)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{10}Q_{c}^{+}(\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}|h|,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}|h|)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}|h|,\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}|f|)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{10}Q^{+}_{c}(\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}|h|,\langle v\rangle^{2k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}|f|)

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=

\displaystyle T_{5}+T_{6}+T_{7}+T_{8}.

We have

\displaystyle T_{5}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}(\|\langle v\rangle^{2k+30}f\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{2k+30}f\|_{L^{p}_{v}})

(5.36)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Similarly,

T_{6}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(5.37)

Next,

\displaystyle T_{7}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{10}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}(\|\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}h\|_{L^{1}_{v}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4k+40}h\|_{L^{p}_{v}})

(5.38)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Similarly,

T_{8}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\alpha}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k+10}\langle v\rangle^{2k+20}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\|\langle x-t\hat{v}\rangle^{k}\langle v\rangle^{4k+50}h\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(5.39)

Gathering all the calculations (
5.32
) to (
5.39
) we have (
5.30
).
The theorem is thus proved.
∎

5.3
Chain rule for the relativistic Boltzmann operator

This subsection is devoted to prove chain rule for the relativistic Boltzmann operator. We prove the following theorem that contains
Theorem

1.2
.

Theorem 5.14
.

For any smooth functions
f,h
, we have

v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}Q_{c}(h,f)=Q_{c}(h,(v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f))+Q_{c}((v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}h),f)-\frac{v_{j}}{v_{0}}Q_{c}(h,f),

(5.40)

and

(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})Q_{c}(h,f)=Q_{c}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)+Q_{c}((v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})h,f).

(5.41)

Proof.

Since
g
is the function of
v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot u
, we have

\partial_{v_{i}}(v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot u)=\frac{v_{i}u_{0}}{v_{0}}-u_{i}=u_{0}\left(\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}-\frac{u_{i}}{u_{0}}\right)=-\frac{u_{0}}{v_{0}}\partial_{u_{i}}(v_{0}u_{0}-v\cdot u)\Longrightarrow\partial_{v_{i}}g=-\frac{u_{0}}{v_{0}}\partial_{u_{i}}g,\,\,[v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}+u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}]g=0.

(5.42)

Moreover,

(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})v_{0}=\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{v_{0}}-\frac{v_{i}v_{j}}{v_{0}}=0,\quad(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})(v\cdot u)=u_{i}v_{j}-u_{j}v_{i}=-(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})\partial_{u_{i}}(v\cdot u).

(5.43)

Thus we obtain from (
5.42
), (
5.43
) that

(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})g=-(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})g
.. For
Q^{+}_{c}
, for simplicity, we define function

\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c):=c\sigma(g,\vartheta)\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})h(v+u-v^{\prime})\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right).

(5.44)

Recall that

\displaystyle Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)=\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{8\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u,

thus using integration by parts in
v^{\prime}
and
u
we have

\displaystyle v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}(\frac{1}{v_{0}})\frac{1}{\bar{g}}+\partial_{v_{i}}(\frac{1}{\bar{g}})\right)\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

(5.45)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v_{0}}{u_{0}}\left(\partial_{v_{i}}(s)\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)+s\partial_{v_{i}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{v_{i}}{v^{2}_{0}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}+\frac{v_{0}^{\prime}}{v_{0}}\partial_{v^{\prime}_{i}}(\frac{1}{\bar{g}})\right)\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-\partial_{u_{i}}(s)\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)+s\frac{1}{u_{0}}v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}\left(\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}\left[f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\left(u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}\left(\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)+v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}\left(\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}(f(v^{\prime}))\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}(u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}+v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}})\left[\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)+Q_{c}^{+}(h,v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f)+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v^{\prime}_{0}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}(u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}+v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}})\left[\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u.

We define opeartor
\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\vcentcolon=u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}+v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}
for simplicity. From (
5.42
) and direct calculations, we have

\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\begin{bmatrix}g\\
\bar{g}\\
\tilde{g}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
0\end{bmatrix},\quad\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})=v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime},\quad\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)(v_{j}+u_{j}-v^{\prime}_{j})=\delta_{ij}(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}),

(5.46)

and we obtain from
Lemma

5.6
and (
5.46
) that
\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\sigma\left(g,\vartheta\right)=0
.

Next, since
\mathpzc u^{\prime}(x)=\delta(x-c)
, we have
\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)=0

and
\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})=\delta(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}-c)(v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime})
. Remind
\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}=g^{2}
, we have
|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2}=|v+u-v^{\prime}|^{2}
. Moreover, we have

v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}-c=0,\quad\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}=0\quad\Longrightarrow v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime}=0,\quad i=1,2,3.

Thus we obtain that

\displaystyle\delta(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}-c)(v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime})\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)=0.

Finally we compute that

\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)h(v+u-v^{\prime})=\sum_{j}\partial_{j}h(v+u-v^{\prime})\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)(v_{j}+u_{j}-v^{\prime}_{j})=(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})\partial_{i}h(v+u-v^{\prime}).

Using again
|v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}|^{2}-c^{2}=|v+u-v^{\prime}|^{2}
, we have
\mathpzc L_{1}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)h(v+u-v^{\prime})=(v+u-v^{\prime})_{0}\partial_{i}h(v+u-v^{\prime})
. Thus

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}(u_{0}\partial_{u_{i}}+v_{0}\partial_{v_{i}}+v_{0}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}})\left[\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u

(5.47)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc u(v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime})[(v+u-v^{\prime})_{0}\partial_{i}h(v+u-v^{\prime})]\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{+}_{c}(v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}h,f).

We next calculate
Q^{-}_{c}
. Recall

Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{cg\sqrt{s}}{4v_{0}u_{0}}\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u,

(5.48)

we compute that

\displaystyle v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}Q_{c}^{-}(h,f)

(5.49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}cg\sqrt{s}\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}(\frac{1}{v_{0}})\frac{1}{4u_{0}}cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\frac{1}{4u_{0}}v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}(cs\sigma(g,\vartheta))h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f)-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)}{4v_{0}u_{0}}h(u)v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}u_{0}\partial_{u_{j}}(cs\sigma(g,\vartheta))h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f)-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)u_{0}\partial_{u_{j}}h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}f)-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q^{-}_{c}(h,f)+Q^{-}_{c}(v_{0}\partial_{v_{j}}h,f),

and (
5.40
) is proved after combining (
5.45
), (
5.47
) and (
5.49
).

2

This completes the proof of
Theorem

1.2
.

Next, to show (
5.41
), we still start by
Q^{+}_{c}
term. Similarly as before, we have

\displaystyle(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})Q^{+}_{c}(h,f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})(\frac{1}{p_{0}})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{p_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p_{j}\partial_{p_{i}}-p_{i}\partial_{p_{j}})(\frac{1}{\bar{g}})\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{p_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{q_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{v_{0}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})(s)}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})\left(\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}-(v_{j}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}-v_{i}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{j}^{\prime}})(\frac{1}{\bar{g}})\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{-(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})(s)}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})\left(\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}(v_{j}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}-v_{i}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{j}^{\prime}})(f(v^{\prime}))\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\bar{g}}\frac{1}{v_{0}^{\prime}}f(v^{\prime})\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}((v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})+(v_{j}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}-v_{i}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{j}^{\prime}})+(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}}))\left[\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{+}_{c}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)+\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{v_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(v^{\prime})}{\bar{g}v_{0}^{\prime}}\textnormal{d}v^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{s}{u_{0}}((v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})+(v_{j}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}-v_{i}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{j}^{\prime}})+(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})).\left[\mathpzc H(v,u,v^{\prime},c)\right]\textnormal{d}u

We define operator
\mathpzc L_{2}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\vcentcolon=v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}}+v_{j}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{i}^{\prime}}-v_{i}^{\prime}\partial_{v_{j}^{\prime}}+u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}}
. From direct calculations,

\displaystyle\mathpzc\displaystyle L_{2}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)\begin{bmatrix}v_{0}+u_{0}-v_{0}^{\prime}\\
\delta\left(\frac{\bar{g}^{2}+\tilde{g}^{2}-g^{2}}{2\bar{g}}\right)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix},\quad\mathpzc L_{2}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)(v_{k}+u_{k}-v_{k}^{\prime})=(v_{j}+u_{j}-v_{j}^{\prime})\delta_{ik}-(v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime})\delta_{jk}.

(5.50)

Thus we obtain from (
5.50
) that

\mathpzc L_{2}\left(v,u,v^{\prime}\right)(f)(v+u-v^{\prime})=(v_{j}+u_{j}-v_{j}^{\prime})(\partial_{i}f)(v+u-v^{\prime})-(v_{i}+u_{i}-v_{i}^{\prime})(\partial_{j}f)(v+u-v^{\prime}),

which implies that

(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})Q_{c}^{+}(h,f)=Q_{c}^{+}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)+Q_{c}^{+}((v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})h,f).

Recall that
(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})g=-(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})g

and (
5.48
), we compute that

\displaystyle(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})Q_{c}^{-}(h,f)

(5.51)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}cg\sqrt{s}\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})(\frac{1}{v_{0}})\frac{1}{4u_{0}}cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})(cs\sigma(g,\vartheta))h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})(cs\sigma(g,\vartheta))h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{v_{0}}(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})(\frac{1}{4u_{0}})cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{1}{4v_{0}u_{0}}cs\sigma(g,\vartheta)(u_{j}\partial_{u_{i}}-u_{i}\partial_{u_{j}})h(u)f(v)\textnormal{d}\omega\textnormal{d}u

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q^{-}_{c}(h,(v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})f)+Q^{-}_{c}((v_{j}\partial_{v_{i}}-v_{i}\partial_{v_{j}})h,f),

so the proof of (
5.41
) is thus finished.
∎

Remark 5.15
.

We emphasize that our results on the chain rule are obtained without assuming the product form
\sigma(g,\vartheta)=|g|^{\gamma-1}\sigma_{0}(\vartheta)
, allowing for more general collision kernels.

Corollary 5.16
.

We have

|\frac{1}{v_{0}}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}v_{0}Q_{c}(f,f))|\lesssim\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha}|Q_{c}(\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{1}}f,\hat{Z}^{\alpha_{2}}f)|.

(5.52)

Proof.

Notice that

|\frac{1}{v_{0}}\hat{Z}^{\beta}(v_{0})|\lesssim 1,\quad|\hat{Z}^{\beta}(\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}})|\lesssim 1,\quad\forall\hat{Z}\in\tilde{\mathbb{K}},\quad\hat{Z}Q_{c}(f,g)=Q_{c}(\hat{Z}f,g)+Q_{c}(f,\hat{Z}g),\quad\hat{Z}=\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{i}},\hat{\Omega}_{ij},S,

and

\hat{\Omega}_{i}Q_{c}(f,g)=Q_{c}(\hat{\Omega}_{i}f,g)+Q_{c}(f,\hat{\Omega}_{i}g)-\frac{v_{i}}{v_{0}}Q_{c}(f,g).

so (
5.52
) follows by
Theorem

5.14
.
∎
